Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出INTENT框架，基于LSTM模型在交叉口场景下提前2秒预测车辆意图（直行、左转、右转），在InD数据集上达到99.71%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升自动驾驶车辆在复杂交互场景（如交叉口、环岛、紧急情况）中对驾驶员意图的理解能力，从而增强行车安全性和敏捷性，避免或减轻灾难性事故。

Method: 提出INTENT框架，采用LSTM模型，在交叉口场景下提前2秒预测车辆意图（直行、左转、右转），并在InD数据集上进行多种模型实验和消融研究。

Result: 在InD数据集上达到99.71%的准确率。

Conclusion: INTENT框架能够高效准确地预测交叉口车辆意图，为自动驾驶系统提供关键决策支持，同时可增强轨迹预测（意图条件化轨迹预测）。

Abstract: Vehicle intention prediction is a pivotal aspect in the agility and safety of autonomous vehicles in all driving scenarios; if genuine enhancement of autonomous vehicles are required, we need to make them adopt human interpretation of driver's intention especially in cases that require a lot of human interaction as well as complex driving behaviors like the ones at intersections, roundabouts and emergency cases such as sudden stops where vehicle intention prediction helps in taking the correct evasive action within a real time period where every second of action makes an impact and can prevent a catastrophe from taking place. In the worst case, it helps minimize the damage and make safety a priority. Intention prediction can also be used to enhance trajectory prediction (intention conditioned trajectory prediction). In this study, The INTENT framework is proposed using LSTM model to predict the vehicle's intention at intersections 2 seconds ahead of the event occurrence to predict whether the cars in intersections are going straight, turning left, or turning right. Various model experiments and ablation study are thoroughly tested on InD dataset achieving 99.71% accuracy.

</details>


### [111] [PolyUQuest: Verifiable Structure-Aware Web RAG over Heterogeneous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08269)
*Ying Liu,Yi Ye,Quanyu Feng,Mingxi Ye,Mingtao Zhang,Haoyang Li,Chen Jason Zhang,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolyUQuest 是一个基于异构图的、结构感知的 Web RAG 框架，通过统一超链接拓扑、DOM 层级结构和跨页面实体关系，实现可验证的检索增强生成，在 PolyU 官网数据集上显著优于现有 RAG 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统将网页视为纯文本，丢失了 HTML 中编码的结构和语义信号，导致检索和生成质量受限。

Method: 构建异构图表征页面间超链接拓扑、页面内 DOM 层级和跨页面实体关系；设计两级路由器将查询分发至三种检索模式（直接块检索、跨页面图遍历、多跳实体推理）；每个引用块携带源页面、标题路径和实体链接，实现完全可验证。

Result: 在包含 4,240 页、31,086 个 DOM 块、29,119 个实体和 37,680 个关系的 PolyU 官网数据集上，PolyUQuest 在答案正确性、覆盖率和忠实度上优于现有 RAG 系统，且显著减少每个查询的 LLM token 消耗。

Conclusion: PolyUQuest 证明了结构感知的 Web RAG 框架在提升检索质量、答案可验证性和效率方面的有效性，并已准备在 PolyU 部署为学生面对面的 QA 服务。

Abstract: Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat web pages as flat text, losing the structural and semantic signals encoded in HTML. We present PolyUQuest, a verifiable, structure-aware web RAG framework built on a heterogeneous graph that unifies hyperlink topology between pages, DOM hierarchy within pages, and entity-relation knowledge across pages. A two-tier router dispatches each query to one of three retrieval modes matched to its structural need, including direct block retrieval, cross-page graph traversal, and multi-hop entity reasoning. Every answer is fully verifiable, as each cited block carries its source page, heading path, and entity links so that users can trace any claim back to its structural evidence. We evaluate on the official websites of the Hong Kong Polytechnic University (PolyU), comprising 4,240 pages, 31,086 DOM blocks, 29,119 entities, and 37,680 relations, together with a multi-type evaluation benchmark. PolyUQuest outperforms existing RAG systems in answer correctness, coverage, and faithfulness, while consuming significantly fewer LLM tokens per query. The demonstration provides an interactive interface for inspecting cited answers, comparing retrieval traces across routing modes, and exploring evidence graph paths. PolyUQuest is being prepared for deployment as a student-facing QA service at PolyU.

</details>


### [112] [Understanding Axes of Difficulty For Long Context Tasks Via PredicateLongBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08284)
*Siddhartha Jain,Ameya Velingker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PredicateLongBench是一个通过寻找满足给定约束的最长连续子序列来压力测试LLM长上下文推理能力的基准，系统性地探索了多个难度轴，发现前沿模型在这些难度扩展时表现挣扎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文评估主要衡量平均性能，许多已饱和或缺乏鲁棒性，缺少系统性地按多个难度轴缩放任务来探测模型表现的方法。

Method: 提出PredicateLongBench基准，要求模型在长输入中找出满足给定谓词/约束（如字典序）的最长连续子序列；设计了完全合成和基于真实文档两条生成管线，并系统探索多个难度轴。

Result: 前沿模型在多个难度轴扩展时表现挣扎，难以很好地完成任务。

Conclusion: 该基准能有效揭示当前长上下文能力的局限性，且任务概念简单，无需LLM生成或评判。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated rapidly improving long-context capabilities, prompting a wave of benchmarks designed to evaluate them. However, existing long-context evaluations - from Needle-in-a-Haystack (NIAH) tests to more recent multi-hop reasoning and summarization tasks - predominantly measure average-case performance, and many are either saturated or lack robustness. Notably absent is a systematic way to probe how models perform as we scale up the difficulty of tasks along various axes. We address this gap by proposing PredicateLongBench, a benchmark that stress-tests long-context reasoning by asking models to identify the longest contiguous subsequence of words in a long input that satisfies given predicates/constraints (e.g., lexicographic ordering), drawn from a broader predicate class. The central innovation of our benchmark is the identification and systematic exploration of multiple different axes of difficulty which test multiple aspects of long context understanding. We provide two complementary generation pipelines - a fully synthetic setup using random word-like strings, and a real-world setup that samples words from natural documents while preserving their distributional properties. We find that frontier models struggle to perform well as we scale up the difficulty of tasks along our axes, demonstrating the utility of our benchmark in understanding the limitations of current long-context capabilities. Furthermore, the tasks in PredicateLongBench, though challenging, are conceptually simple and do not require LLM-based generations or judges.

</details>


### [113] [Psychological Competence as a Missing Dimension in AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08285)
*Marcos Economides,Paul M. Sacher,Samuel Salzer,Alexis Michelle Abellar,Fendi Tsim,Antoine Ferrère*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"心理胜任力"作为AI评估中缺失的关键维度，指人向AI系统支持用户认知、情绪解释和行为决策的能力，并构建了概念框架与评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估框架主要关注技术性能（如准确性、鲁棒性、推理能力和策略合规性），但对于直接与用户进行自然语言交互的系统而言，这些指标不足以评估其对用户认知、情绪、信念、信任和决策产生的实际影响。因此需要引入心理胜任力这一新维度。

Method: 基于行为科学和人-AI交互研究，提出心理胜任力的概念框架，界定其核心域（包括框架效应、语气、感知权威性、响应性、不确定性处理和对话引导等交互属性），并描述通过场景化探针、结构化人工评估和模型辅助评估等方法进行评估的路径。

Result: 明确了心理胜任力的定义（人向AI系统以适合用户、情境和交互目的的方式支持用户认知、情绪解释和行为决策的能力）及其边界，提出了评估方法框架，但未提出具体的基准测试。

Conclusion: 心理胜任力应成为模型提供者、部署组织、研究者和监管机构在人向AI系统评估中的核心考量因素。

Abstract: Current AI evaluation frameworks focus primarily on technical performance, including accuracy, robustness, reasoning ability, and policy compliance. These measures remain essential, but they are not sufficient for systems that interact directly with users through natural language. Human-facing AI systems are increasingly used as advisors, coaches, tutors, and companions. In these roles, their responses can shape how users reason, interpret emotions, form beliefs, calibrate trust, and make decisions. The relevant unit of evaluation is therefore not only the model, but the human-AI interaction. This paper introduces psychological competence as a missing dimension in AI evaluation. We define psychological competence as the capacity of a human-facing AI system to support user cognition, emotional interpretation, and behavioral decision-making in ways that are appropriate to the user, context, and purpose of the interaction. This includes interaction properties such as framing, tone, perceived authority, responsiveness, uncertainty handling, and conversational guidance. Existing evaluation approaches capture parts of this problem but rarely assess these psychological effects directly. Drawing on behavioral science and human-AI interaction research, we outline a conceptual framework for psychological competence and its core domains. Rather than proposing a specific benchmark, we define the construct, clarify its boundaries, and describe how it may be assessed through scenario-based probes, structured human evaluation, and model-assisted evaluation methods. We argue that psychological competence should become a core consideration for model providers, deploying organizations, researchers, and regulators concerned with the real-world effects of human-facing AI systems.

</details>


### [114] [Blind-Spots-Bench: Evaluating Blind Spots in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08317)
*Matteo Santelmo,Xiuying Wei,Israa Fakih,Felix Bauer,Juan Garcia Giraldo,Chengkun Li,Etienne Bamas,Emmanuel Abbé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 blind-spots-bench，一个通过收集学生设计的简单但现代AI难以解决的任务来暴露当前模型盲点的基准测试，实验发现闭源前沿模型在整体上优于开源模型约10%，且没有任何模型在所有任务类型上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试难以全面反映现代AI模型的真实缺陷，模型在人类认为简单的任务上仍然频繁失败（如字符串操作、绘制五条腿的狗），因此需要设计专门暴露这些盲点的诊断性基准。

Method: 从AI课程中收集学生提出的原始问题，经过清理和结构化标注后形成包含235个样本的数据集，提出适配的任务分类体系，并开发自动化评测流水线来评估包括开源/闭源语言模型、视觉语言模型和图像生成模型在内的多种模型。

Result: 闭源前沿模型在 blind-spots-bench 上整体领先开源模型约10%的准确率差距，尽管它们在已有基准上性能相当；不同模型在不同任务类型上各有所长，没有任何模型在所有任务上占据主导地位；部分任务对所有评估模型均构成挑战。

Conclusion: blind-spots-bench 能够有效作为诊断性压力测试，揭示当前现代AI模型中具体的薄弱环节，凸显了现有基准测试在衡量模型真实能力方面的不足。

Abstract: Modern AI models achieve strong performance on many established benchmarks, yet they still fail on tasks that humans find almost trivial, such as manipulating a string or drawing a dog with five legs. These examples suggest that existing benchmarks may under-measure persistent blind spots in current systems. We introduce $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$, a benchmark designed to expose such blind spots through tasks that appear simple for humans but remain challenging for modern AI. We collect raw questions from students in an AI course, clean and annotate them with structured reference solutions, and propose a task taxonomy tailored to the resulting dataset of 235 samples. We further develop an automated grading pipeline to evaluate a wide range of models, including open-weight and closed-source language, vision-language, and image-generation models. Our analysis on $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ reveals that closed-source frontier models can substantially outperform open-weight models with even $\approx10\%$ gap, even when they attain comparable performance on existing benchmarks. A more fine-grained analysis shows that no single model dominates across all task types, and that some tasks remain challenging for all evaluated models. These results highlight the value of $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ as a diagnostic stress test for identifying concrete weaknesses in current modern models.

</details>


### [115] [MobiDiff: Semantic-Aware Multi-Channel Discrete Diffusion for Human Mobility Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08357)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ximiao Li,Taichi Liu,Desheng Zhang,Yuan Tian,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobiDiff是一个端到端离散扩散框架，通过多通道语义骨架去噪直接生成人类移动数据，避免了现有方法中昂贵的插值和粗到细实现流程，在保持生成保真度和隐私保护的同时，推理速度比GeoGen平均快5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实人类移动数据获取成本高且因隐私问题难以共享，现有基于扩散的方法虽能合成移动模式，但依赖于连续或潜在时空轨迹，无法原生建模具有显式区域、活动、时间和间隔结构的离散语义事件。

Method: MobiDiff将每次签到事件分解为空间、活动和时间三个通道，采用结构化的事件级、组级和通道级掩码策略，在离散空间中直接对多通道语义骨架进行去噪，联合捕获轨迹级移动模式和事件内部依赖关系。

Result: 在亚特兰大、波士顿和西雅图三个真实数据集上，MobiDiff有效保留了轨迹长度和时间间隔分布，在更广泛的移动统计数据上保持竞争力；推理速度平均比GeoGen快5.3倍。

Conclusion: 离散扩散为合成移动数据生成提供了一种可解释且高效的框架。

Abstract: Human mobility data are essential for transportation optimization, urban planning, and resource allocation, yet real-world mobility data are costly to collect and difficult to share due to privacy concerns. Recent diffusion-based methods have shown promise in synthesizing realistic mobility patterns, but they typically rely on continuous or latent spatio-temporal traces, limiting their ability to natively model discrete semantic events with explicit region, activity, time, and interval structures. To address this issue, we introduce MobiDiff, an end-to-end discrete diffusion framework that efficiently generates mobility data by directly denoising multi-channel semantic skeletons, avoiding the costly interpolation, latent trace construction, and coarse-to-fine realization pipelines widely used in existing diffusion-based methods. Specifically, MobiDiff decomposes each human check-in event into spatial, activity, and temporal channels, and employs structured event-, group-, and channel-level masking to jointly capture trajectory-level mobility patterns and within-event dependencies. We evaluate generation fidelity, privacy-preserving, and efficiency on three large-scale real-world datasets from Atlanta, Boston, and Seattle. Results show that MobiDiff effectively preserves trajectory length and temporal interval distributions while remaining competitive across broader mobility statistics; it is also much faster than state-of-the-art methods, e.g., 5.3$\times$ faster than GeoGen on average during inference. These findings suggest that discrete diffusion offers an interpretable and efficient framework for synthetic mobility data generation.

</details>


### [116] [FedOPAL: One-Shot Federated Learning via Analytic Visual Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08368)
*Lingyu Qiu,Daniela Annunziata,Stefano Izzo,Fabio Giampaolo,Francesco Piccialli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对非独立同分布数据下分析式联邦学习特征流形错位问题，本文提出FedOPAL框架，通过视觉提示作为特征修正器，结合局部近端约束主动将异构数据特征分布修正至线性可分空间，从而满足分析式联邦学习的理论假设。实验表明FedOPAL在零服务器训练成本下，性能显著优于原始分析方法，且精度媲美最先进的迭代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在边缘智能部署中，通信带宽成为联邦学习扩展性的核心瓶颈。单次联邦学习虽减少通信轮次，但现有迭代微调或知识蒸馏方法存在服务器端计算成本高、超参数敏感等问题。分析式联邦学习利用最小二乘闭式解实现高效无梯度聚合，但在非独立同分布数据环境下，其静态特征假设失效，导致特征流形错位，严重损害模型性能。

Method: 提出FedOPAL框架：将视觉提示（Visual Prompts）作为特征修正器（Feature Rectifiers），通过局部近端约束（Local Proximal Constraints）主动将异构数据的特征分布修正至线性可分空间，从而满足分析式联邦学习的理论假设，实现高效的梯度无关聚合。

Result: FedOPAL在多个基准上显著优于原始分析方法；在保持零服务器训练成本的同时，精度达到与最先进迭代方法相当的水平。

Conclusion: FedOPAL为解决非独立同分布数据下分析式联邦学习的特征流形错位问题提供了一种新范式，在边缘端大模型高效协作中实现了零服务器训练成本与SOTA精度的平衡，具有工程应用价值。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of basic models in edge intelligence, communication bandwidth has become a core bottleneck restricting the scalability of federated learning. Although one-shot federated learning alleviates this problem by minimizing communication rounds, existing iterative fine-tuning or knowledge distillation methods still face challenges such as high server-side computational costs and hyperparameter sensitivity. Analytical federated learning achieves efficient gradientfree aggregation using least-squares closed-form solutions, but in environments with non-independent and identically distributed data, its static feature assumptions fail, leading to feature manifold misalignment and severely impairing model performance. To address this contradiction, this paper proposes the FedOPAL framework. This framework adapts the visual prompts as feature rectifiers, actively correcting the feature distribution of heterogeneous data to a linearly separable space by applying local proximal constraints, thereby satisfying the theoretical assumptions of analytical federated learning. Experimental results show that FedOPAL not only significantly outperforms the original analytical methods on several benchmarks, but also achieves accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art iterative methods while maintaining zero server-side training costs, providing a new engineering paradigm for efficient collaboration of large models on the edge.

</details>


### [117] [Towards Mechanistically Understanding Why Memorized Knowledge Fails to Generalize in Large Language Model Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08393)
*Lu Dai,Ziyang Rao,Yili Wang,Hanqing Wang,Hao Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM微调新知识时存在"知道-使用鸿沟"（能记忆但无法用于推理），通过自修补干预技术发现这是由于知识回路的错位（内部存在记忆表征但未路由至有效计算层），并设计简单启发式策略可恢复58-75%的泛化失败性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调LLM注入新知识时，模型能快速记忆新事实却无法将其用于下游推理任务，这一"知道-使用鸿沟"现象缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 1) 形式化定义"Knowing-Using Gap"，包含准确率差距和记忆-泛化之间的时间滞后；2) 提出自修补（self-patching）干预技术，通过定位并重定位激活表征来观测知识在模型内部的空间渗透动态；3) 提出知识回路错位假说（knowledge-circuit misalignment hypothesis）解释该现象。

Result: 自修补实验支持知识回路错位假说：记忆表征存在于模型内部但未被路由至计算有效层；基于此诊断设计的简单启发式策略可恢复58-75%的oracle性能上限。

Conclusion: LLM微调新知识时的泛化失败源于内部表征与计算路由的错位，而非知识本身缺失；通过干预表征路由可以显著缓解该问题，且该发现具有跨领域鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs to inject new knowledge faces a critical challenge: LLMs can quickly memorize new facts, yet fail to use them for downstream reasoning tasks. We formalize this failure as the \textit{\textbf{Knowing--Using Gap}}, characterized by an accuracy gap and a temporal lag between memorization and generalization. To understand this phenomenon, we fine-tune LLMs with unseen knowledge and monitor the spatial permeation dynamics of the knowledge internally using a novel intervention technique called self-patching. Self-patching identifies activation locations where relocating representations substantially improves failed generalization cases. These results are consistent with a knowledge-circuit misalignment hypothesis: memorized representations can exist internally but may not be routed to computation-effective layers. To demonstrate the practicality of this diagnostic finding, we design a simple heuristic strategy which recovers 58--75\% of the oracle headroom in generalization failure. Experiments are done cross-domain for the robustness of this finding.

</details>


### [118] [Game Theory Driven Multi-Agent Framework Mitigates Language Model Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08403)
*Runzhe Liu,Biquan Bie,Zihao Wang,Yuchao Ma,Yexin Liu,Xinghai Li,Harry Yang,Wenbo Yang,Jinzhe Cao,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-Frame框架，通过贝叶斯与团队博弈原理驱动的自适应多智能体系统，合成了专门语料库（363,045条思维链和199,589个问答对），训练出7B参数模型OmniChem，在专用基准和ChemBench上达到GPT-4o mini水平，幻觉率较基座模型降低79.46%，并在分子设计和合成规划中展现高级能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级大语言模型在基于规则的科学领域中容易模仿语言模式而非复现公理推理，频繁产生幻觉，严重限制了其应用。

Method: G-Frame：一种整合贝叶斯和团队博弈原理的自适应多智能体框架，通过结构化推理强制内化领域约束，构建自动闭环实现高质量数据合成与模型训练。

Result: OmniChem（7B模型）在定制基准和ChemBench上达到GPT-4o mini的性能水平，幻觉率相较于基座架构降低79.46%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个利用自适应多智能体克服固有推理缺陷的可扩展范式，为加速科学专业知识发现提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: The application of lightweight Large Language Models in rule-based scientific domains remains severely limited by their tendency to mimic linguistic patterns rather than reproduce axiomatic reasoning, causing frequent hallucinations. Here, we show that G-Frame, an adaptive multi-agent framework integrating Bayesian and team game principles, establishes an automated closed-loop for high-quality data synthesis and model training. By forcing the internalization of domain constraints through structured reasoning, we synthesized a specialized corpus of 363,045 chains-of-thought and 199,589 question-answer pairs. The resulting 7B model OmniChem achieves performance parity with GPT 4o mini on custom benchmarks and ChemBench while exhibiting a 79.46% reduction in hallucinations relative to its base architecture. We further demonstrate the advanced capabilities of OmniChem in molecular design and synthesis planning. This work establishes a scalable paradigm utilizing adaptive multi-agents to overcome inherent reasoning deficiencies, offering a feasible pathway for accelerating knowledge discovery in specialized scientific fields.

</details>


### [119] [OmniFood-Bench: Evaluating VLMs for Nutrient Reasoning and Personalized Health Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08423)
*Qian Jiang,Zhecheng Shi,Jingpu Yang,Zirui Song,Miao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniFood-Bench，一个从 MM-Food-100K 数据集构建的综合基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在食品系统中的三项渐进能力：基础感知、定量推理和安全关键建议。实验发现即使最先进模型也存在严重的"语义-物理鸿沟"：在菜品命名上接近人类，但在质量估计和安全医疗建议上表现灾难性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注粗粒度分类任务（如食物类别识别），无法评估真实饮食管理所需的复杂推理链——从识别隐藏成分、估计物理质量到合成安全关键医疗建议的能力。需要建立更严格的基准来评估自主代理在公共卫生部署中的可信度。

Method: 从 MM-Food-100K 数据集构建 OmniFood-Bench 基准，设计三个渐进评估维度：基础感知（成分与烹饪方法）、定量推理（份量大小与营养分析）、安全关键建议（疾病特定推荐）。评估六个先进 VLM，包括 gpt-5.1、gemini-3-flash、qwen3-vl-8B 等。

Result: 模型在菜品命名上达到接近人类精度，但在质量估计上出现灾难性失败，且经常为高风险糖尿病患者的饮食配置产生幻觉性建议。揭示了"语义-物理鸿沟"——视觉语义理解与物理/营养参数推理之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: OmniFood-Bench 为部署于公共卫生领域的自主代理建立了可信度评估的严格标准。当前 VLM 在需要定量推理和安全关键判断的实际饮食管理场景中仍不可靠。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into critical infrastructure promises to revolutionize
  personalized healthcare and dietary management. However, in the domain of food systems, autonomous agents face a
  unique and persistent challenge: the "Systemic Information Asymmetry" between visual appearance and intrinsic
  nutritional composition. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on coarse-grained classification tasks, such as food
  category recognition, which fail to evaluate the intricate reasoning chain required for real-world dietary management
  -- specifically, the ability to traverse from identifying hidden ingredients to estimating physical mass, and finally
  synthesizing safety-critical medical advice. In this paper, we introduce OmniFood-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark
  constructed from the MM-Food-100K dataset. Unlike previous works, OmniFood-Bench evaluates VLMs across three
  progressive capabilities: Basic Perception (Ingredients & Cooking Methods), Quantitative Reasoning (Portion Size &
  Nutritional Profiling), and Safety-Critical Advisory (Disease-Specific Recommendations). We evaluate six
  state-of-the-art VLMs, including gpt-5.1, gemini-3-flash, and qwen3-vl-8B. Our extensive experiments reveal a
  startling "Semantic-Physical Gap": while models achieve near-human accuracy in naming dishes, they exhibit
  catastrophic failure in mass estimation and frequently hallucinate benign advice for high-risk diabetic profiles. This
  work establishes a rigorous standard for trustworthiness in autonomous agents deployed for public health. The code
  and datasets are available in: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OmniFood-Bench-7D0B

</details>


### [120] [Applying JEPA-Style Predictive Learning to JA4-Derived Network Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08465)
*Javier Izquierdo,Aygul Zagidullina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了JA4-JEPA，一种基于Transformer的模型，采用JEPA风格的预测性自监督学习范式（而非重建输入），在JA4、JA4H、JA4S、JA4X等网络指纹子字段上进行训练。模型在39,416个保留样本上取得了0.9899的余弦相似度和0.9220的kNN准确率，证明JEPA式预测学习能够从JA4衍生指纹中产生有用的嵌入表示，即使训练数据中存在视图不完整重叠的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索JEPA（图像/视频领域成功的预测性潜在表示学习范式）是否同样适用于紧凑的网络指纹（JA4系列）表示学习，以解决传统重建式自监督方法在网络指纹任务中的局限性。

Method: 构建JA4-JEPA，一个基于Transformer的模型，在来自JA4DB和CIC-IDS-2017的约39.7K样本上，对JA4、JA4H、JA4S、JA4X四个子字段进行训练。采用JEPA风格的目标：匹配潜在预测与目标编码器输出，而非重建原始输入。评估使用冻结的kNN探针进行协议族分类（TLS、DNS、SSH）。

Result: 在39,416个保留样本上，模型达到0.9899的余弦相似度和0.9220的kNN准确率。

Conclusion: JEPA风格的预测性学习能够从JA4衍生的网络指纹中产生有效的嵌入表示，即使在训练数据中各视图（子字段）存在不完整重叠的情况下也能取得良好性能。

Abstract: I-JEPA and V-JEPA learn by matching latent predictions to target encoder outputs rather than regenerating the original input, and this has worked well for images and video. We explore whether the same objective works for compact network fingerprints. We built JA4-JEPA, a Transformer-based model trained on JA4, JA4H, JA4S, and JA4X subfields drawn from JA4DB and CIC-IDS- 2017. The training data combines roughly 397K samples from both sources, though no single sample contains all four view families. We evaluated the learned representations with a frozen kNN probe on protocol-family classification across TLS, DNS, and SSH. On 39,416 heldout samples the model achieved a cosine similarity of 0.9899 and a kNN accuracy of 0.9220. These results indicate that JEPA-style predictive learning can produce useful embeddings from JA4-derived fingerprints, even with incomplete view overlap across sources.
  Keywords: JA4, network fingerprinting, JEPA, predictive representation learning, self-supervised learning

</details>


### [121] [Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) for Adaptive Semantic Modeling in Biomedical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08490)
*Bharathwaj Vijayakumar,Sahana K. Varadaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种漂移感知时序图重连（DATGR）框架，通过逻辑更新规则动态调整共现边权重来建模概念演化，在BIOMRC数据集上相比静态基线AUROC提升约0.066，且保持计算高效性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学语言随新发现快速演化，传统文本模型（静态嵌入、共现图）无法捕捉语义漂移，导致检索和知识发现任务性能下降。

Method: 提出DATGR框架，使用轻量级、反馈驱动的边重连机制，通过逻辑更新规则动态调整共现边的权重以估计语义漂移，无需为每个时间片重新训练嵌入。

Result: 在BIOMRC数据集上，DATGR相比静态基线AUROC提升约0.066（0.699 vs. 0.633），AUPRC保持可比（0.738 vs. 0.744），表明漂移感知适应在不损失精度的情况下提升了链接预测召回率。

Conclusion: 边级自适应能有效捕捉演化生物医学文本中的时序语义变化，同时保持计算高效性和可解释性。

Abstract: Biomedical language evolves rapidly as new discoveries emerge, causing traditional text models to lose semantic fidelity over time. Static embeddings and co-occurrence graphs cannot capture such evolution, leading to performance degradation in retrieval and knowledge discovery tasks. This paper introduces a Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) framework that models concept evolution by dynamically updating co-occurrence edges based on estimated semantic drift. Instead of retraining embeddings for each time slice, DATGR performs lightweight, feedback-driven rewiring using a logistic update rule applied to edge weights. Evaluated on the Biomedical Multi-Relation Corpus (BIOMRC), the method achieved a mean Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) improvement of approximately 0.066 absolute difference (0.699 vs. 0.633) over a static baseline. Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC) remained comparable (0.738 vs. 0.744), showing that drift-aware adaptation enhances link-prediction recall without a loss in precision. These results demonstrate that edge-level adaptation effectively captures temporal semantic change in evolving biomedical text while remaining computationally efficient and interpretable.

</details>


### [122] [AI-guided stimuli discovery and generation to optimize facial emotion perception studies in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08533)
*Kushin Mukherjee,Na Yeon Kim,Maren Wehrheim,Ralph Adolphs,Kohitij Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型引导的框架，通过训练人群特异性人工神经网络模型，发现自闭症与神经典型成人在面部情绪感知上的差异集中在少数诊断性表情上，并利用生成对抗网络对刺激进行变换，从而优化行为表型分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究在自闭症与神经典型成人面部情绪感知差异上的结果不一致，作者推测这种变异性可能源于刺激层面的稀疏性，即差异集中在少数面孔上而非均匀分布。需要一种更敏感、可靠且机制信息丰富的行为测定方法来揭示群体差异。

Method: (1) 训练人群特异性人工神经网络模型（ANN）预测每个图像层面的情绪判断；(2) 利用模型选择能最大化群体分离的新面孔；(3) 在独立队列中验证模型选择图像比随机匹配图像产生更大的行为差异；(4) 使用生成对抗网络（GAN）将诊断性图像转换为趋向群体一致性更高的版本，并在表型匹配验证中检验其效果。

Result: 模型选择的图像在独立队列中产生比随机图像更大的行为差异；经GAN变换后的合成图像相比原始图像减少了群体间的行为分离。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个模型引导的框架，能够发现和变换揭示群体特异性感知差异的刺激，表明行为表型分析可以超越对固定刺激集进行平均的传统方法，转向能够识别神经多样性感知在何种条件下发散或趋同的优化测定。

Abstract: Understanding perceptual differences between autistic and neurotypical adults requires behavioral assays that are sensitive, reliable, and mechanistically informative. Facial emotion perception is a useful test case because group differences have been reported, but findings vary across studies. Here we show that this variability may reflect image-level sparsity: autistic-neurotypical differences in emotion judgments were concentrated in a small subset of diagnostic facial expressions rather than spread uniformly across stimuli. We trained population-specific artificial neural network models to predict image-level judgments for autistic and neurotypical participants, then used these models to select novel faces predicted to maximize group separation. In an independent cohort, model-selected images produced larger behavioral differences than matched random images. We then used the same models with a generative adversarial network to transform diagnostic images toward greater predicted group agreement. In phenotype-matched validation, synthesized images reduced behavioral separation relative to their matched originals. These results establish a model-guided framework for discovering and transforming stimuli that reveal population-specific perceptual differences. More broadly, they show how behavioral phenotyping can move beyond averaging across fixed stimulus sets toward optimized assays that identify the conditions under which neurodivergent perception diverges or converges.

</details>


### [123] [CommuniWave:A Machine Learning Model for Quantifying the Degree of Temporary Informal Behavior in Urban Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08554)
*Hongye Yang,Shien Liu,Zhihao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CommuniWave机器学习模型，通过整合BCN、YLX和BEM三个模块，从街道视频中检测和量化城市社区的 Informal Behavior 程度（DIB），生成波动图以支持动态监测和精细化管理，从而增强社区韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市社区规划常采用自上而下的方式，缺乏量化居民非正式行为的有效指标，导致规划与实际行为频繁冲突，亟需一种能够动态监测和量化非正式行为的方法来提升社区韧性。

Method: CommuniWave模型集成三个模块：基于mmaction2的行为捕获网络（BCN）、自开发的YOLOv10模型（YLX）、以及使用随机森林的行为评估模型（BEM），从街道视频中生成非正式行为程度（DIB）波动图。

Result: 模型能够从街道视频生成DIB波动图，实现对非正式行为的动态监测，支持城市管理者做出精细化决策，从而提升社区整体韧性。

Conclusion: CommuniWave为城市管理者提供了一种数据驱动的工具，通过量化非正式行为来弥补自上而下规划的不足，有助于增强城市社区在面对复杂性和不确定性时的韧性。

Abstract: For urban managers and designers, improving the functional attributes of urban communities to enhance territorial resilience in the face of complexity and uncertainty is crucial. Currently, community planning often follows a top-down approach and lacks effective metrics to quantify informal behaviors of residents, leading to frequent conflicts with original plans. This study introduces CommuniWave, a machine learning model designed to efficiently detect and quantify the Degree of Informal Behavior (DIB) in urban communities. The model integrates a Behavior Capture Net (BCN) based on mmaction2, a self-developed YOLOv10 model (YLX), and a Behavior Eval Model (BEM) using random forest. Ultimately, by generating DIB fluctuation charts from street videos, the model facilitates dynamic monitoring, supporting urban managers in making refined decisions to enhance the overall resilience of communities.

</details>


### [124] [SHAP-Weighted Cross-Modal Expert Fusion for Emotion and Sentiment Recognition: Evidence and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08573)
*Adis Alihodzic,Selma Skopljakovic Hubljar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了XAI引导的自适应融合（XGAF），一种基于TreeSHAP归因幅度的树状混合专家融合方法，重点研究了当专家特征维度不相等时SHAP归因约简的影响。实验表明，sum-abs约简在MELD（7类情感识别，Transformer变体达0.5983 WF1，与早期融合0.6018无显著差异，p=1.000）和CMU-MOSEI（3类情感识别，达0.6519 WF1，略超早期融合0.6485）上均取得了接近或略优于早期融合的性能，显著优于晚期融合。消融实验表明主要增益来自跨模态专家（尤其是三模态专家），而非复杂的逐样本路由机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合机制存在根本性权衡：早期融合准确但整体化，晚期融合模块化但可能丢失跨模态交互。本文旨在通过XAI引导的混合专家方法实现兼顾准确性和模块化的多模态融合，并系统分析SHAP归因约简策略对高维专家特征的影响。

Method: 采用XAI引导的自适应融合（XGAF），构建基于TreeSHAP归因幅度的树状混合专家模型，包含单模态和跨模态专家，样本级权重由SHAP值决定。对比三种SHAP约简策略（mean-abs、median-abs、sum-abs），在MELD和CMU-MOSEI数据集上使用三种人脸序列聚合器进行实验。

Result: 1) sum-abs XGAF在MELD上达到0.5983 WF1（早期融合0.6018，晚期融合0.4598），McNemar检验与早期融合无显著差异（p=1.000），显著优于晚期融合（p<0.0001）；2) 在CMU-MOSEI上达到0.6519 WF1，略超早期融合0.6485；3) 消融实验显示主要增益来自跨模态专家，特别是三模态专家；4) mean-abs和median-abs权重近乎均匀分布，而sum-abs权重集中于三模态专家。

Conclusion: XAI引导的融合方法能够在不牺牲模块化优势的前提下达到与早期融合相当甚至更优的性能，关键在于选择合适的SHAP约简策略（sum-abs）和跨模态专家设计，而非复杂的逐样本路由机制。本文提供了关于SHAP约简、专家维度和跨模态专家设计如何影响模块化多模态融合的透明实证分析。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion and sentiment recognition is commonly addressed by early fusion, which concatenates modalities before classification, or late fusion, which combines independently trained unimodal predictors. Early fusion can be accurate but monolithic, while late fusion is modular but may lose cross-modal interactions. This paper revisits XAI-guided adaptive fusion (\xgaf), a tree-based mixture of unimodal and cross-modal experts whose sample-level weights are derived from TreeSHAP attribution magnitudes. We focus on the effect of SHAP attribution reduction when experts have unequal feature dimensionalities. In this setting, mean-abs and median-abs reductions can suppress high-dimensional cross-modal experts, whereas sum-abs reduction preserves total attribution mass. On MELD 7-class emotion recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} nearly matches early fusion across three face-sequence aggregators; the Transformer variant reaches 0.5983 \wf{}, compared with 0.6018 for early fusion and 0.4598 for probability-average late fusion. McNemar testing shows no significant difference between sum-abs \xgaf{} and early fusion on MELD ($p=1.000$), while \xgaf{} remains significantly better than late fusion ($p<0.0001$). On CMU-MOSEI 3-class sentiment recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} reaches 0.6519 \wf{}, slightly exceeding early fusion (0.6485) and late fusion (0.5696). Ablation studies show that the main gain comes from adding cross-modal experts, especially the trimodal expert, rather than from complex per-sample routing. Diagnostics further show that mean-abs and median-abs weights are nearly uniform, while sum-abs weights concentrate on the trimodal expert. Thus, the main contribution is a transparent empirical analysis of how SHAP reduction, expert dimensionality, and cross-modal expert design affect modular multimodal fusion.

</details>


### [125] [Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08602)
*Peng Cui,Jitao Wang,Siyan Xue,Yao Huang,Haoming Xia,Dong Li,Dengxiang Liu,Weilin Wang,Liping Liu,Leida Zhang,Yunfu Cui,Tao Peng,Daolin Ji,Haitao Zhao,Wei Zhang,Xiaojuan Wang,Weijie Ma,Zongren Ding,Jinlong Li,Yuan Ding,Jiajing Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Chengkun Yang,Ziyue Huang,Jiaqi Liu,Fusheng Liu,Yang Zhou,Xiaojuan Wang,Zhongquan Sun,Shiyun Bao,Xiaojun Wang,Ming Yang,Guangxin Li,Bin Shu,Yong Liao,Hongxuan Li,Yao Tang,Shizhong Yang,Yongyi Zeng,Yufeng Yuan,Yinpeng Dong,Jihui Hao,Jun Zhu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HCC-STAR，一种基于大语言模型的临床决策支持系统，能够从电子病历叙事文本中自动进行肝癌分期、推荐循证治疗方案并提供个体化生存预测。在6688例患者的多中心验证中，该模型在治疗推荐和风险分层上超越了临床指南（BCLC/CNLC）及GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro等模型，遵循其推荐的中位生存期（51个月）显著优于指南（29-32个月），且经盲法评估被肝胆专家认定为可信赖，并能辅助医生提高决策准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肝癌指南和分期系统存在粗粒度分类、忽视电子病历中临床上下文及分期内异质性等问题，亟需能够从真实世界EMR中自动提取信息并实现精准分期、治疗推荐和预后评估的智能化决策支持系统。

Method: 基于约30,000例SEER数据经临床验证的提示词增强工作流生成EMR式叙事训练语料；设计知识对齐推理框架，采用步骤可验证的复合奖励进行优化；在12家医院6,668例患者的多中心队列中评估，并与BCLC/CNLC指南及GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro等模型对比。

Result: HCC-STAR在治疗推荐和风险分层上达到SOTA；遵循其推荐的假设性总生存分析中位生存期51个月，显著优于BCLC（29个月）和CNLC（32个月）；盲法评估中肝胆专科医生认可其推理和循证依据；在治疗准确性上超越住院医师和主治医师，作为辅助工具可帮助医生更快做出更准确的决策。

Conclusion: HCC-STAR可作为肝癌风险分层和精准治疗中可靠、可验证的临床决策支持系统，具有改善患者预后的潜力。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a common malignancy and a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. Current guidelines and staging systems provide coarse categories, but often miss within-stage heterogeneity and the clinical context in electronic medical records (EMRs). We present HCC-STAR (Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs risk score-based staging, ranked guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curated about 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expanded them into EMR-style narrative training data using a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. On this corpus, we developed a knowledge-aligned reasoning framework optimized with a step-verifiable composite reward, moving beyond text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. In a multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals in China, HCC-STAR achieved state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, including GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analysis showed a median survival of 51 months under adherence to HCC-STAR recommendations, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC. In clinician-centric evaluations, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rated HCC-STAR's reasoning and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpassed resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and helped physicians make more accurate decisions faster when used as an assistant. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.

</details>


### [126] [The complexities of patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08625)
*João Matos,Olivia Buege,Donny Cheung,Gary S. Collins,Paula Dhiman,Nan Li,Bingyu Mao,Benjamin W. Nelson,Michail Ouroutzoglou,Paul Varghese,Jonathan Amar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了2,053个真实患者与健康聊天机器人的对话，开发了分别建模临床内容、情绪状态、会话策略和沟通风格的患者模拟器，并通过图灵测试验证了其真实性。使用5种不同的患者角色和1,164个临床医生评分案例评估了四个大语言模型的紧急分级性能，发现沟通风格会显著改变分诊结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健康聊天机器人的开发和评估主要依赖合作、表达清晰的模拟患者，忽略了真实世界中患者沟通模式和情绪表达的广泛多样性，可能导致系统在真实部署中表现不佳并加剧健康不公平。

Method: 分析了2,053个真实患者与聊天机器人的对话；开发了分别建模临床内容、情绪状态、会话策略和沟通风格的患者模拟器；用15名人类评分员进行图灵测试评估模拟真实性；使用5种患者角色和1,164个临床医生评分案例评估四个LLM的紧急分级性能。

Result: 模拟对话与真实对话几乎无法区分，人类评分员识别准确率仅55%；沟通风格显著改变了分诊结果。

Conclusion: 以患者为中心的健康聊天机器人必须适应沟通多样性；为理想化而非真实交互设计的系统在真实世界部署时可能表现不佳并放大健康差异。

Abstract: Consumer-facing health chatbots powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for symptom assessment. However, chatbot development and evaluation often rely on cooperative, articulate, simulated patients. We analysed 2,053 real patient-chatbot conversations and found that communication patterns and expression of emotions vary widely across users. We developed a patient simulator that separately models clinical content, emotional state, conversational strategy, and communication style. In a Turing-inspired evaluation of realism with 15 human graders, simulated conversations were nearly indistinguishable from real ones, with human graders achieving an accuracy of 55%. We used five distinct patient personae, across 1,164 clinician-graded cases, to evaluate the performance of four LLMs in urgency assessment. We found that communication style can significantly alter triage outcomes. Patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence must accommodate communication diversity: systems designed for idealised, rather than realistic, interactions risk underperforming and amplifying health disparities when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [127] [Formal Mechanisms for Market Stability in Self-Interested Agent Societies: A Marketplace Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08652)
*Eugene Ng Yi Sheng,Bingquan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过多智能体市场模拟实验（18个DeepSeek-V3 LLM代理），研究了在重复社会困境中，何种正式机制叠加于无限制通信之上能够维持市场稳定，并测试了这些机制的对抗鲁棒性。结果表明，**调解机制（Mediation）**在8种条件中表现最佳，即使面对经过迭代提示优化的对抗性攻击（最佳攻击使诚实代理效用降低13.3%），仍能维持市场正效用，呈现出“可弯折但不可断裂”的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复社会困境中，自利代理在没有约束的情况下倾向于背叛，导致合作收益崩溃。现有研究缺乏对**何种形式机制**能够叠加于无限制通信之上维持市场稳定，以及这些机制**对对抗性攻击的弹性**的系统性实证研究。

Method: 构建了一个多智能体市场模拟环境：18个LLM代理（DeepSeek-V3）具有互补生产专长，在约束社交网络中交易以获取效用。实验分两阶段：（1）在200轮中，对8种机制条件进行渐进式“巨魔（troll）”注入比较，识别最佳机制；（2）对最佳机制（调解）进行对抗性红队测试，使用迭代提示优化的LLM驱动巨魔进行攻击。

Result: （1）机制比较阶段：**调解机制**在8种条件中表现最优；（2）对抗红队测试阶段：最优攻击（v6版本）使诚实代理效用降低13.3%，但**无法导致市场崩溃**。调解机制即使在持续的对抗压力下仍能支持市场恢复。

Conclusion: 调解机制具有对抗鲁棒性——能够在对优化攻击下维持正的诚实代理效用，呈现出“可弯折但不可断裂”的特性。论文定义了对抗鲁棒性为机制在优化攻击下维持正向效用能力，并证实调解机制满足这一标准。

Abstract: Self-interested agents, left unconstrained, tend toward defection in repeated social dilemmas, causing cooperative gains from trade to collapse. This paper investigates what formal mechanisms, layered on top of unrestricted communication, are sufficient for a society of such agents to maintain market stability, and how resilient those mechanisms are to adversarial attack. We instantiate the research question as a multi-agent marketplace simulation where 18 LLM agents (DeepSeek-V3) with complementary production specialties must trade within a constrained social network to obtain utility. We conduct two experimental phases: (1) a mechanism comparison across eight conditions under progressive troll injection over 200 rounds, identifying Mediation as the top-performing mechanism; and (2) adversarial red-teaming of Mediation using iteratively prompt-optimised LLM-driven trolls, finding that the best attack (v6) reduces honest-agent utility by 13.3% but cannot collapse the market. Mediation enables recovery even under sustained adversarial pressure. We define adversarial robustness as a mechanism's ability to sustain positive honest-agent utility under optimised attack, and find that Mediation is robust: it can be bent but not broken.

</details>


### [128] [SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08681)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SolarChain-Eval基准，用于在去中心化能源市场中评估可信赖的经济智能体，通过物理约束和LLM规划/审计机制，揭示效用-安全性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在能源市场中可能利用无效物理数据、制造虚假流动性、产生不稳定治理决策，现有评估缺乏对可信赖性的全面考量。

Method: 将市场治理建模为Gymnasium兼容的MDP；设计多维度评估指标（市场效用、物理安全、滑点、动作平滑性、空间公平性、可审计性）；引入LLM规划/审计层进行动作边界定义和高风险动作审查；通过结构化日志记录干预全过程。

Result: 静态/随机/短视/RL/RL+LLM策略实验显示效用-安全性权衡；RL智能体提升效用但产生不安全行为；无物理惩罚时奖励最大化智能体会利用无效发电并增加虚假流动性；LLM规划/审计可缓解部分风险但无法弥补错误奖励函数。

Conclusion: 可信赖的智能体评估需要物理约束和透明的干预痕迹，仅靠LLM审计不足以修正奖励函数设计缺陷。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.

</details>


### [129] [Remember When It Matters: Proactive Memory Agent for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08716)
*Yifan Wu,Lizhu Zhang,Yuhang Zhou,Mingyi Wang,Bo Peng,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"行为状态衰减"(Behavioral State Decay)概念，指长程任务中决策相关信息随时间步推移被埋没或超出上下文窗口而无法影响决策的问题。为解决此问题，作者设计了独立于行动智能体的记忆智能体模块，该模块主动更新结构化记忆库并选择性注入记忆提示，在Terminal-Bench 2.0和τ²-Bench上分别提升8.3和6.8个百分点pass@1。同时训练了基于SETA数据集的Qwen3.5-27B开源记忆策略模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中，决策相关信息分散在逐步扩展的轨迹中，随着轨迹增长，任务要求、环境事实、先前尝试、诊断信息和未完成子目标等关键信息会被深埋在上下文窗口或推出窗口之外，导致无法在需要时影响决策。现有方法依赖于被动检索，缺乏主动干预机制。

Method: 提出一个独立的记忆智能体模块，与未修改的行动智能体并行运行。该模块从近期轨迹更新结构化记忆库，并自主决定是否注入基于记忆的提示（reminder）或保持静默。该模块为即插即用设计，兼容前沿行动智能体和现有智能体框架。此外，在SETA数据上使用SFT和GRPO训练Qwen3.5-27B作为开源记忆策略模型。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0和τ²-Bench上，该模块对强弱行动智能体均有提升，Terminal-Bench上提升+8.3个百分点，τ²-Bench上提升+6.8个百分点。消融实验表明选择性干预优于被动记忆库暴露、始终注入、仅顾问指导和通用检索等方法。GRPO训练在验证奖励上有提升，并实现了部分向Terminal-Bench的迁移。

Conclusion: 将记忆定位为主动干预机制而非被动检索，通过选择性注入记忆提示有效缓解行为状态衰减问题。该方法即插即用，对多种行动智能体均有显著提升，并初步探索了开源权重记忆策略的训练路径。

Abstract: In long-horizon tasks, decision-relevant state is often scattered across an expanding trajectory, while the action agent must surface it and act. As trajectories grow, task requirements, environment facts, prior attempts, diagnoses, and open subgoals can be buried in the context window or pushed beyond it, failing to influence decisions when needed. We call this failure mode "behavioral state decay". We study memory as an active intervention mechanism rather than passive retrieval. A separate memory agent runs alongside an unmodified action agent, updating a structured memory bank from the recent trajectory and deciding whether to inject a memory-grounded reminder or remain silent. The module is plug-and-play with frontier action agents and existing agent harnesses. Across Terminal-Bench 2.0 and $τ^2$-Bench, it improves pass@1 for both weaker and stronger action agents, with gains of +8.3 pp on Terminal-Bench and +6.8 pp on $τ^2$-Bench. Ablations show that selective intervention outperforms passive bank exposure, always-on injection, advisor-only guidance, and general retrieval. As an early step toward open-weight memory policies, we train Qwen3.5-27B on SETA using SFT and GRPO, improving validation reward and achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.

</details>


### [130] [The Illusion of Equivalency: Statistical Characterization of Quantization Effects in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08734)
*Baha Rababah,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Carson K. Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 后训练量化在LLM部署中广泛使用，但现有评估仅依赖准确率和困惑度。本文提出决策级指标"正确一致性"，并发现中等量化下即使任务性能看似保持，模型行为已出现明显分歧，揭示基础模型与量化模型之间存在"等价幻觉"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化评估仅依赖准确率和困惑度，但这些指标无法捕捉量化引发的行为变化，导致对模型等价性的错误认知。

Method: 提出"正确一致性"（correctness agreement）这一决策级指标，度量基础模型与量化变体之间正确预测的重叠程度；将量化视为注意力权重的结构算子，使用统计和分布度量量化逐层失真。

Result: 中等量化下行为分歧已出现；低比特宽度存在非线性断裂点；query和key投影比value和output投影对量化更敏感。

Conclusion: 传统性能指标造成基础模型与量化模型等价的错觉，需引入行为级评估以更全面衡量量化影响。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is widely used to deploy large language models in resource-constrained settings, yet its evaluation relies almost exclusively on accuracy and perplexity. We show that these metrics fail to capture behavioral changes induced by quantization. We introduce correctness agreement, a decision-level metric that measures overlap in correct predictions between a base model and its quantized variants, independent of absolute accuracy. Across multiple models and quantization schemes from 8-bit to 2-bit, we find that behavioral divergence emerges under moderate quantization even when task performance appears preserved. To explain this effect, we analyze quantization as a structural operator on attention weights and quantify layer-wise distortions using statistical and distributional measures. Our results reveal non-linear breakpoints at low bit-widths and show that query and key projections are consistently more sensitive than value and output projections. These findings expose an illusion of equivalence between base and quantized models and motivate behavioral evaluation beyond conventional performance metrics.

</details>


### [131] [Workflow as Knowledge: Semantic Persistence for LLM-Mediated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08740)
*Emanuele Quinto,Carlo Andrea Rozzi,Francesco Zanitti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Lisp启发的、语言无关的概念模型，将工作流定义、实例、推理记录等视为持久化知识对象，核心语义区分确定性计算（derive）与LLM中介推理（infer），实现工作流本身作为可检查、可恢复、可审查的知识对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM应用虽使用显式工作流处理工具调用、检索、分支等，但缺乏统一的概念模型来表征工作流及其产物的持久化语义，工作流仅产生知识但自身不作为可检查的知识对象。

Method: 借鉴Lisp的符号形式、对象标识和活镜像概念，提出语言无关的概念模型。核心是区分derive（确定性计算）和infer（LLM中介推理），并将工作流定义、实例、推理记录、上下文快照、依赖关系均表示为共享知识基底中的持久化知识对象。

Result: 建立了语义持久性的初步概念框架：工作流不仅是产生知识的执行过程，其自身也可作为可检查、可恢复、可审查的知识对象来表示。形式化迁移语义留作未来工作。

Conclusion: 该概念模型为LLM工作流的可观察性、可恢复性和可审查性提供了理论基础，但形式化语义仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly use explicit workflows for tool use, retrieval, branching, checkpointing, and human approval. Existing workflow systems already address many execution concerns. This paper proposes a Lisp-inspired but language-independent conceptual model: symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking are used as explanatory lenses, not implementation commitments. In this model, workflow definitions, workflow instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependency relations are represented as persistent knowledge objects in a shared knowledge substrate. Its central semantic distinction is between derive and infer: derive is deterministic computation over available state; infer is mediated LLM judgment under declared context and executor-controlled capability policy.
  The result is a preliminary conceptual account of semantic persistence: workflows do not merely produce knowledge and leave traces, but can themselves be represented as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge objects, while formal transition semantics remain future work.

</details>


### [132] [AUTOPILOT VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Incident-Centric Dashcam Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08745)
*Siddharth Damodharan,Radhika Gupta,Ali Alshami,Ryan Rabinowitz,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AUTOPILOT-VQA，一个面向行车记录仪视频的以事故为中心的视觉问答基准，用于评估视觉-语言模型在自动驾驶安全关键场景中的推理能力，涵盖天气、交通环境、道路布局、事故可避免性等多维度安全类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在自动驾驶任务（场景理解、决策、轨迹预测等）中取得进展，但缺乏对模型能否可靠推理安全关键事件的评估。现有基准多聚焦于目标识别，未能系统评估模型在安全相关场景中的时间敏感推理能力。

Method: 构建以真实驾驶事故和近事故为中心的行车记录仪视频VQA基准，设计围绕事故的结构化问题，覆盖天气光照、交通环境、道路布局、路面状态、标识、涉事实体、事故发生、撞击位置及可避免性推理等多样化的安全类别。

Result: 该基准作为CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT竞赛的一部分发布，提供了评估自动驾驶系统在不同场景下可靠性的标准化基准，推动更可解释、鲁棒和安全意识的视觉-语言系统发展。

Conclusion: AUTOPILOT-VQA推动评估从目标识别向时间上可归因的、安全感知的推理转变，为自动驾驶领域的安全可靠性评估提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models, Large Language Models, and Multimodal Large Language Models have improved autonomous driving tasks such as scene understanding, decision making, trajectory prediction, and visual question answering. However, evaluating whether these models can reliably reason about safety-critical incidents remains challenging. To address this gap, we present AUTOPILOT-VQA, an incident-centric visual question answering benchmark for dashcam video understanding. The dataset evaluates different systems through structured questions designed around real-world driving incidents and near-incidents. The benchmark covers diverse safety-relevant categories, including weather and lighting conditions, traffic environment, road layout, road surface state, signage, involved entities, accident occurrence, impact location, and avoidability-related reasoning. By requiring models to answer grounded questions about both contextual scene properties and event-level incident details, AUTOPILOT-VQA moves beyond object recognition toward temporally grounded, safety-aware reasoning. The dataset is released as part of the AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 competition and provides a standardized benchmark for assessing the reliability of autonomous driving systems in different scenarios. Our benchmark support developments for more interpretable, robust, and safety-conscious vision-language systems for real-world autonomous driving.

</details>


### [133] [Using AI-based Learning Assistants in Higher Education: A Large-Scale Descriptive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08748)
*Kristina Schaaff,Quintus Stierstorfer,Valerie Heckel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于77,543名远程学习者的客观日志数据，本研究对AI学习助手Syntea在高等教育中的使用模式进行了大规模描述性分析，发现该工具已融入学习者的学习常规，但使用情况因人口统计学和结构背景而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于教育聊天机器人的研究大多依赖小样本和自述调查数据，缺乏基于实际使用行为的大规模证据，因此本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用大规模描述性分析方法，基于77,543名远程学习者的客观日志数据，考察了性别、年龄组、学习集群、学位和学习模式等维度上的使用模式差异。

Result: Syntea已嵌入许多学习者的学习常规中，但使用情况在不同人口统计学和结构背景（如性别、年龄、学位等）下存在差异。

Conclusion: 本研究为AI学习支持系统的进一步发展提供了实证基础，并贡献了高等教育中教育聊天机器人使用的大规模分析。

Abstract: In this study, we present a large-scale descriptive analysis of the use of an AI-based learning assistant (Syntea) in higher education. Based on objective log data from 77,543 students enrolled in distance studies, we examine usage patterns across gender, age group, study cluster, degree, and study mode. To date, existing research on educational chatbots has largely relied on comparatively small samples and self-reported survey data, while large-scale evidence on actual usage behavior remains limited. Our findings show that Syntea is already embedded in the study routines of many learners, but that usage differs across demographic and structural contexts. By identifying these patterns, our study provides an empirical basis for the further development of AI-based learning support and contributes a large-scale analysis of educational chatbot usage in higher education.

</details>


### [134] [Ideas Have Genomes: Benchmarking Scientific Lineage Reasoning and Lineage-Grounded Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08758)
*Yifan Zhou,Qihao Yang,Yan Li,Donggang Li,Xiru Hu,Hokin Deng,Ziyang Gong,Xuanyi Zhou,Huacan Wang,Xiangchao Yan,Wanghan Xu,Wenlong Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Yue Zhou,Yifan Yang,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench)，一个用于评估 AI 系统科学谱系推理与谱系约束下想法生成能力的基准。该基准包含 1,961 条谱系轨迹、1,085 个 Idea Genome 对象和 920 个 GenomeDiff 记录，覆盖 10 个科学领域。实验表明最强系统在谱系推理上仅达 27.3% 精确准确率，且谱系上下文会重新排序系统排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少评估 AI 系统能否遵循科学思想中的继承结构（如修复已知局限、重组先前工作），因此需要专门针对科学谱系推理和谱系约束下想法生成的基准。

Method: 构建 IdeaGene 框架：将论文表示为带类型和证据的 Idea Genome 对象，用 GenomeDiff 记录六种演化动力学（继承、突变、丢失、外部引入、新插入）。构建 1,961 条谱系轨迹、1,085 个 Genome 对象和 920 个 GenomeDiff 记录。设计 IG-Exam（42 任务类型，1,029 实例）评估闭式谱系推理，IG-Arena 用 Population-Evolution Score (PES) 评估谱系约束下的想法生成。

Result: 在 14 个 LLM 驱动的科学系统上实验发现：最强系统谱系推理精确准确率仅 27.3%；结构化谱系上下文会重新排序系统排名而非均匀提升所有系统，暴露出组合瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前 AI 系统在科学谱系推理和谱系约束下的想法生成方面存在显著不足，需要更好的方法来处理科学思想的继承与组合结构。

Abstract: Scientific ideas rarely start from a blank page. They inherit mechanisms, repair known limitations, and recombine pieces of earlier work, much like biological genomes. Current benchmarks still say little about whether AI systems can follow this inheritance structure. We present IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench), a benchmark for scientific lineage reasoning and lineage-grounded idea generation. IG-Bench is organized around the IdeaGene framework: each paper or proposal is represented as a set of minimal, typed, evidence-grounded Idea Genome objects, and a GenomeDiff aligns these objects to record inheritance, mutation, loss, external import, and novel insertion under six operational evolutionary dynamics. The benchmark contains 1,961 golden lineage traces, 1,085 curated Idea Genome objects, and 920 pairwise GenomeDiff records across 10 scientific domains. It supports two evaluations. IG-Exam (42 task types, 1,029 instances) tests closed-form lineage reasoning across Idea Genome abstraction, inheritance tracing, evolutionary reasoning, and lineage verification. IG-Arena evaluates generation with a lineage-conditioned Population-Evolution Score(PES), asking whether a proposal can be inserted as a coherent descendant of a given lineage population: it should inherit the right Idea Genome objects, vary meaningfully from nearby work, and offer selection value for future research. Experiments on 14 LLM-based scientists expose a compositional bottleneck. The strongest system reaches only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context reshuffles system rankings rather than helping every participant uniformly.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [SASGeo: Stability-Aware Semantic Map Localization for GNSS-Denied UAVs -- A Framework and Synthetic Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07737)
*Natalia Trukhina,Vadim Vashkelis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAS框架，利用道路、建筑物等持久性语义结构实现GNSS拒止环境下的无人机绝对定位，通过语义栅格对齐、关系图证据、特征稳定性等模块实现跨视角图像检索。在220次随机化试验中，全局语义描述符Recall@1为58.6%，空间语义匹配变体达94.5-95.5%，证明语义几何优于全局描述符，但各空间变体间无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS拒止环境下的无人机需要绝对位置修正以约束视觉惯性里程计的漂移，现有跨视角图像检索方法受季节、光照、视角、地图时效和传感器模态等因素影响较大。

Method: 提出SAS语义地图定位框架：1)语义栅格对齐；2)关系图证据；3)特征稳定性与地理区分度；4)显式正例/矛盾/未知观测建模；5)完整性感知的模糊位置拒绝机制。采用具体的加权和决策模型，进行220次含旋转、尺度变化、裁剪、遮挡、地图变化和语义干扰的随机化检索试验。

Result: 全局语义描述符Recall@1为58.6%，空间语义匹配变体达94.5-95.5%。Wilson 95%置信区间显示全局描述符与空间变体间显著分离，但各空间变体间存在重叠，表明语义几何的效果得到验证，但各模块的独立贡献尚未明确区分。

Conclusion: 结构性语义几何能在受控跨视角扰动下区分位置，但尚未经过实飞验证。需进一步解决别名混淆、地图老化等更具挑战性的测试，以及更严格的拒绝机制验证。

Abstract: GNSS-denied unmanned aerial vehicles require occasional absolute position fixes to bound the drift of visual-inertial odometry. Cross-view image retrieval can provide such fixes, but raw appearance is sensitive to season, illumination, viewpoint, map age, and sensor modality. We propose \sas, a semantic map-localization framework that represents the environment through persistent structures such as roads, buildings, waterways, railways, intersections, and field boundaries. The method combines semantic raster alignment, relational graph evidence, feature stability and geographic distinctiveness, explicit positive/contradictory/unknown observations, and integrity-aware rejection of ambiguous fixes. Unlike a broad architecture-only proposal, this paper specifies concrete weighting and decision models and reports a reproducible synthetic proof of concept. In 220 randomized retrieval trials with rotation, scale changes, partial crops, occlusion, simulated map changes, and hard semantic decoys, a global semantic descriptor achieved 58.6\% Recall@1, while spatial semantic matching variants achieved 94.5-95.5%. Wilson 95\% intervals separate the global descriptor from the spatial variants but overlap among the spatial variants, so the experiment supports semantic geometry rather than a definitive benefit from each proposed module. The preliminary experiment does not validate real-flight navigation; rather, it demonstrates that structured semantic geometry can discriminate locations under controlled cross-view perturbations and identifies the harder aliasing, map-aging, and rejection tests required next.

</details>


### [136] [Physics-Guided Biomechanical Gait Adaptation for Humanoid Locomotion on Extreme Sloped Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07830)
*Xuanyu Chen,Mohan Liu,Dengchen Mei,Zhihao Gu,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Haiyue Zhu,Shijun Yan,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumoSlope两阶段框架，通过物理引导的零力矩点正则化（Stage I）和生物力学步态适配器（Stage II），实现了无模型强化学习在陡坡（最高62.7%/32.1°）上的鲁棒人形机器人盲走，有效避免了低重心蜷曲步态退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模型强化学习在平坦地形上取得了令人印象深刻的人形机器人 locomotion 成果，但陡坡控制尚未被充分探索。与平坦或离散地形不同，斜坡地形施加持续的引力偏差，要求同时实现稳定性和姿态控制。在通用奖励公式下，策略可能收敛到缓慢、保守的低重心（CoM）蜷曲步态。

Method: 提出HumoSlope两阶段物理引导框架：Stage I引入坡度自适应零力矩点（ZMP）正则化器，在局部倾斜支撑面而非世界水平参考面上进行评估，建立地形一致的平衡先验；Stage II引入生物力学斜坡步态适配器（BSGA），利用提取的宏观地形描述符作为特权训练信号，动态门控软奖励先验，根据估计的坡度几何调节CoM高度和下肢协调——鼓励髋主导的上坡推进和膝主导的下坡制动。部署的actor完全基于本体感觉，无需在线外部感知。

Result: 广泛的Sim-to-Real实验表明，该框架有效缓解了姿态退化，实现了在户外草地斜坡上盲走、连续穿越最高达62.7%（32.1°）的坡度。

Conclusion: 验证了一种物理引导的方法来实现具有挑战性的斜坡地形适应，使无模型强化学习在陡坡人形机器人控制上取得显著进步。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning has enabled impressive humanoid locomotion; however, control on steep slopes remains largely unexplored. Unlike flat or discrete terrains, sloped terrains impose a persistent gravitational bias that demands simultaneous stability and posture control. Consequently, under generic reward formulations, policies can converge to slow, conservative low-center-of-mass (CoM) crouched gaits.
  In this work, we propose a novel two-stage physics-guided framework, dubbed HumoSlope, dedicated to robust humanoid locomotion on diverse sloped terrains. Specifically, Stage I establishes a terrain-consistent balance prior by introducing a slope-adaptive Zero Moment Point (ZMP) regularizer evaluated directly on the local inclined support plane rather than a world-horizontal reference. To prevent the resulting policy from defaulting to a crouched posture, Stage II introduces the Biomechanical Slope Gait Adapter (BSGA). Utilizing extracted macroscopic terrain descriptors as privileged, training-only signals, BSGA dynamically gates soft reward priors to modulate CoM height and lower-limb coordination based on the estimated slope geometry -- encouraging hip-dominant uphill propulsion and knee-oriented downhill braking. Crucially, the deployed actor remains entirely proprioceptive, requiring no online exteroceptive sensing.
  Extensive Sim-to-Real experiments demonstrate that our framework effectively mitigates posture degeneration and enables blind, continuous traversal of outdoor grass slopes up to 62.7% ($32.1^\circ$), validating a physics-guided approach to challenging slope terrain adaptation.

</details>


### [137] [Shift & Drift: A Zero-Shot Benchmark for Generalizable and Robust Autonomous Driving Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07844)
*Alessandro Canevaro,Hang Yu,Julian Schmidt,Peizheng Li,Silvan Lindner,Wilhelm Stork,Georg Martius,Julian Jordan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Shift & Drift 双轨基准测试，分别评估运动规划器在语义分布偏移（利用航拍数据转换到新城市拓扑）和状态分布偏移（对自车动力学注入随机扰动）下的泛化与鲁棒性。实验发现模仿学习方法在分布内表现优异，但在语义偏移下显著退化且易受积累误差影响，而基于强化学习的方法展现出更优雅的性能衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环运动规划器在大规模数据集上表现良好，但其对新颖城市拓扑的泛化能力以及在执行扰动后的恢复机制尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建双轨基准：(1) 语义偏移轨道——将航拍 DeepScenario Open 3D 数据集转换为 nuPlan 仿真框架，实现零样本评估；(2) 状态分布偏移轨道——对自车动力学注入随机扰动。系统评估多种规划范式在两类偏移下的失效模式。

Result: 模仿学习方法在分布内基准上得分高，但在语义偏移下（尤其是行人密集环境）显著失败，且在时间相关的执行噪声下持续漂移；基于强化学习的方法在两个轨道上均保持更优的安全性和进度指标。

Conclusion: 发现模仿保真度与闭环韧性之间存在经验性权衡，为社区提供了评估可靠部署进展的严格基准。

Abstract: While closed-loop motion planners trained on large-scale, object-level datasets, e.g., nuPlan, demonstrate strong in-distribution (ID) performance, their generalization to novel urban topologies and recovery mechanisms following execution perturbations remain under-explored. To address this, we present Shift & Drift, a novel dual-track benchmark designed to rigorously stress-test motion planners across two critical axes of distribution shift: (1) The Semantic Shift Track leverages a novel conversion pipeline that transforms the aerial, DeepScenario Open 3D dataset into the nuPlan simulation framework. This enables zero-shot evaluation of planners trained on North American and Singaporean data against 1,182 scenarios spanning four German cities and the US city of San Francisco featuring dense pedestrian-cyclist interactions. (2) The State-Distribution Drift Track injects stochastic perturbations into the ego vehicle's dynamics to quantify robustness against compounding execution errors. Based on this, we systematically evaluate the failure modes of diverse planning paradigms under semantic and state-distribution shifts. While imitation learning methods achieve high scores in ID benchmarks, they exhibit significant failures under semantic shift, particularly in pedestrian-dense environments, and suffer from persistent drift when subjected to temporally correlated actuation noise. In contrast, the evaluated reinforcement-learning-based planner demonstrates more graceful degradation, maintaining higher safety and progress metrics across both tracks. Our findings reveal an empirical trade-off between imitation fidelity and closed-loop resilience, providing the community with a rigorous benchmark to evaluate progress toward reliable deployment.

</details>


### [138] [Time-to-Collision Based Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance Using Pretrained Vision Models for Robots in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07885)
*Erik Jagnandan,Mulugeta Haile,Gregory Barber,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅依赖真实世界数据、无需模拟训练和大量机器人专属数据的视觉动态避障方法，通过预训练单目深度估计（UniDepth）和特征点跟踪（SuperPoint+SuperGlue）管线计算碰撞时间（TTC），选择2D运动基元实现避障，在M3ED数据集上验证了数据高效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化户外环境中动态障碍物避障面临挑战，现有方法依赖大规模仿真数据训练或大量机器人专属数据，存在sim-to-real迁移问题和数据效率低下问题。

Method: 1) 使用UniDepth预训练模型从RGB视频生成密集深度图；2) 扩展SuperPoint+SuperGlue特征匹配管线，跨长帧序列跟踪关键点；3) 利用相机内参和预测深度将2D关键点投影到3D；4) 对3D关键点进行光束法平差优化；5) 计算每个关键点的碰撞时间（TTC）；6) 选择2D地面运动基元，使机器人远离最小TTC关键点的最近接近点。

Result: 在M3ED数据集上，识别TTC<1秒帧的精确率0.49、召回率0.38；真正例中84%正确生成避障方向；22个物理障碍中检测到20个存在TTC<1秒的帧；仅需74秒数据进行超参数调优。

Conclusion: 该方法完全消除模型训练需求，展现出极高的数据效率和可解释性，能泛化到多种障碍物类型，避免了sim-to-real问题。

Abstract: Dynamic obstacle avoidance in unstructured outdoor environments remains a critical challenge for autonomous mobile robots, particularly when large-scale robot-specific training data and simulation-based policies are impractical. We present a data-efficient, interpretable method for vision-based dynamic obstacle avoidance that operates entirely on real-world data, avoiding the sim-to-real transfer problem inherent in simulation-trained policies. Our approach leverages UniDepth, a large pretrained monocular depth estimation model, to produce dense depth maps from RGB video without requiring stereo cameras or LiDAR at inference time. Dynamic obstacle avoidance is achieved by extending the SuperPoint and SuperGlue feature correspondence pipeline to track keypoints across long frame sequences, projecting their 2D pixel-space positions into 3D using camera intrinsics and predicted depth, running bundle adjustment initialized from these 3D keypoints, and computing per-keypoint time-to-collision (TTC). A 2D motion primitive in the ground plane is then selected to move the robot away from the closest point of approach of the minimum-TTC keypoint. Evaluated on real-world data from the M3ED dataset, our pipeline achieves a precision of 0.49 and a recall of 0.38 in identifying frames with a ground truth TTC below 1 second, and correctly generates the evasive motion direction in 84\% of true positive detections. Crucially, it detects at least one frame with TTC less than 1 second for 20 out of 22 unique physical obstacles present in our test sequences. Unlike end-to-end learned methods that demand thousands of hours of robot-specific training data, our approach eliminates model training entirely, requiring only 74 seconds of data for hyperparameter tuning. This demonstrates exceptional data efficiency while preserving interpretable and generalizable behavior across diverse obstacle types.

</details>


### [139] [STEMbot: A Compliant Robot for Under-Canopy Plant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07873)
*Zachary Charlick,Nilay Roy Choudhury,Haoyu Ma,Xiaonan Huang,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STEMbot是一种微型爬行机器人系统，能够在植物冠层下自主导航，通过集成PIN-SLAM定位与语义八叉树建图、流形约束A*路径规划等方法，实现对叶背和茎秆上的害虫进行早期检测，在7-33mm的茎秆上可靠爬行，重建精度（Chamfer距离）小于1cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机农业的规模化受限于害虫监测的劳动成本，现有无人机和地面机器人只能从上方或侧面监测，而许多害虫生活在叶片背面或茎秆上，被发现时已造成显著损害，因此需要能够在植物冠层下攀爬的微型机器人实现早期害虫检测。

Method: STEMbot系统集成三个核心模块：1) 基于全几何PIN-SLAM的定位与语义八叉树地图构建；2) 流形约束A*路径规划器，结合射线追踪目标指定实现分支感知遍历；3) 微型爬行机器人硬件平台。

Result: 在7-33mm直径的茎秆上实现可靠爬行，在四种不同植物标本上完成自主导航，几何重建平均Chamfer距离小于1cm（与离线摄影测量基线对比），证明系统保持了全局一致的里程计。

Conclusion: STEMbot首次实现了带有板载感知的微型爬行机器人在植物冠层下的自主导航，能够在复杂分支环境中进行精确的定位与建图，为有机农业中隐蔽害虫的早期自动化检测提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: The scalability of organic agriculture is partially limited by the labor costs associated with monitoring for pests. While drones and rovers are well-suited for agricultural monitoring from above or next to plants, many pests live on the underside of leaves or on plant stems, making them detectable only after they have caused significant damage. To enable early pest detection we present STEMbot, a miniature climbing robot system designed for autonomous navigation under plant canopies. Unlike existing climbing platforms that lack on-board perception or are restricted to unbranched vertical trunks, STEMbot integrates a fully geometric PIN-SLAM pipeline with a semantic OcTree to achieve robust localization and mapping while climbing the plant. To plan STEMbot's motion we propose a manifold-constrained A* planner along with ray-tracing goal specification to enable branch-aware traversal and the inspection of occluded targets. We validate our system through hardware experiments, demonstrating reliable traversal of stems ranging from 7-33mm and autonomous navigation across four distinct plant specimens. Quantitative evaluations show that our system achieves high-fidelity geometric reconstructions with an average Chamfer distance of less than 1cm relative to an offline photogrammetry baseline, confirming that STEMbot maintains the globally consistent odometry needed for autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [140] [Monocular Vision Based Control Framework for Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07897)
*Shail Jadav,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单目视觉的统一抓取框架，仅使用RGB输入和位置控制夹爪，通过结合开放词汇检测、图像分割、边界感知点分配、实时点跟踪和单目深度估计，以及基于语言描述的刚度估计模型，实现对软硬物体的稳定抓取，并在Franka Emika Research 3机械臂上以生菜、马苏里拉奶酪、牛角包、纸巾和硬塑料瓶等物体进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取方法通常分别处理软体或刚性物体，且常依赖触觉传感、物体特定模型或专用夹爪，缺乏统一的、传感器高效的通用抓取方案。

Method: 提出统一单目视觉抓取框架，包含：(1) 开放词汇物体检测与图像分割；(2) 边界感知点分配与实时点跟踪；(3) 单目深度估计恢复物体运动与几何；(4) 基于语言描述的刚度估计模型提供抓取策略先验；(5) 对软物体使用Procrustes差异度量作为形变视觉代理来调整抓取，对刚性物体通过跟踪点距离缩放控制夹爪宽度。

Result: 在真实世界拾放实验中，对生菜、马苏里拉奶酪、牛角包、纸巾和硬塑料瓶等不同机械属性的物体实现了稳定抓取，证明框架能仅凭视觉反馈统一处理软硬物体。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了实用、传感器高效且可推广的抓取方案，尤其适用于食品处理和家务操作场景。

Abstract: Grasping in unstructured environments requires handling objects with widely different mechanical properties, from soft and deformable items to rigid everyday objects. Most existing approaches address these categories separately and often rely on tactile sensing, object-specific models, or specialized grippers. In this paper, we present a unified monocular vision-based grasping framework that targets both soft and rigid objects within a single control pipeline, using only RGB input and a position-controlled gripper. The proposed system combines open-vocabulary object detection, image segmentation, boundary-aware point assignment, real-time point tracking, and monocular depth estimation to recover object motion and geometry from visual observations. A key component of the framework is a language-based stiffness estimation model that infers an object's expected compliance from its semantic description and provides an object-level prior for selecting the grasping strategy before contact. For deformable objects, grasp adaptation is governed by a Procrustes-based dissimilarity measure computed from tracked keypoints, which acts as a visual proxy for deformation. For rigid objects, the gripper width is regulated through the scaling of tracked point distances. We validate the proposed method in real-world pick-and-place experiments on a Franka Emika Research 3 arm using objects with substantially different mechanical properties, including lettuce, fresh mozzarella cheese, croissants, paper towels, and hard plastic bottles. Results demonstrate that the framework achieves stable grasping across both soft and rigid objects using visual feedback alone, highlighting a practical, sensor-efficient, and generalizable approach for food handling and household manipulation.

</details>


### [141] [Towards Soft Robotic Exogloves for Musculoskeletal Manipulation to Reduce Pain and Spasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07958)
*Antonia Salluce,Maeryn Erdheim,Gailen Davis,Lauren H. Sullivan,Max-William Kanz,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款兼具辅助运动与按摩放松功能的软体机器人外手套，通过个性化软体气动执行器设计，为手部痉挛患者提供舒适贴合与针对性治疗，初步实验验证了其可行性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球约1200万人受手部痉挛及疼痛困扰，现有软体机器人外手套仅能辅助运动或缓解疼痛，缺乏同时解决两个问题的方案。

Method: 设计了个性化软体气动执行器，包括指背执行器（贴合手部拓扑、均匀分布力）、指腹执行器（可压缩以适应痉挛手指）、手掌执行器（立体光刻3D打印复杂几何结构），并通过有限元建模优化几何形状以减少应力。

Result: 指背执行器实现全贴合接触与最大力分布；指腹执行器成功压缩适应痉挛手指；手掌执行器成功3D打印；整体手套通过试点用户验证了舒适性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该软体外手套初步证明了同时解决手部痉挛患者的疼痛管理与运动辅助的可行性，为个性化软体康复设备提供了新思路。

Abstract: Hand spasticity and resulting pain affect 12 million people worldwide, including stroke survivors, arthritis patients, and those with other muscle and nerve deficiencies. Soft robotic exogloves are being introduced to help patients enhance mobility or manage pain; however, there are no current solutions that address both pain and mobility. We present preliminary development of a soft robotic exoglove that both aids in mobility and administers massage-like compression to relax spastic muscles. The glove consists of soft pneumatic actuators that are personalized to an individual's hand topology and kinematics, allowing for optimal conformability and targeted mobility. Novel soft actuators were designed, analyzed, fabricated, assembled into an exoglove, and experimentally tested. Actuators were 3D modeled and analyzed with finite element modeling under pressures of 100 and 200 kPa. Geometries were optimized to minimize stress before fabrication and testing. A dorsal finger actuator was successfully customized to a participant's hand topology, providing full conformal contact and maximal force distribution. A ventral finger actuator was successfully fabricated that can be drastically compressed in size to fit into the tight space of a hyperflexed spastic finger. A palmar actuator was successfully printed with stereolithography, showing potential for 3D-printed soft actuators with more complex geometries. The glove was assembled and successfully worn by a pilot user to validate initial findings in comfort and effectiveness.

</details>


### [142] [Soft Robotic Exogloves for Dexterous Mobility -- Towards Personalized Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07968)
*Paul Dela Cruz,Mostafa Mo. Massoud,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑扫描和硅胶模铸的个性化气动软体机器人外手套，通过有限元分析和气压控制实验验证了其对手部解剖结构的精确适配性，能够实现手指关节的精确驱动，为灵巧操作和神经肌肉康复奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体康复外手套多采用标准化尺寸，缺乏个体化适配，限制了精细手部运动功能的康复效果。

Method: 采用拓扑扫描获取用户手部形态，通过硅胶模铸制造个性化气动执行器；利用有限元分析评估执行器弯曲及人机交互接触力；通过气压控制实验实现手指静态与动态屈曲。

Result: 拓扑扫描可精确适配手部解剖结构；有限元分析揭示了人机接触力分布，表明关节活动度充足但近节指骨存在非理想压缩；气压控制可精确驱动MCP和PIP关节；放松应变限制层可改善执行器-手指关节对齐。

Conclusion: 该工作在结构适形性、关节拓扑、人机接触建模和时间相关的驱动-变形轮廓方面实现了对手部的个性化适配，为外手套设计优化和灵巧操作辅助提供了基础。

Abstract: Soft robotic exogloves can provide hand rehabilitation and assistance. Fitting these gloves often relies on standardized measurements not tailored to the individual, limiting their effectiveness, especially for fine articulation necessary for dexterous manipulation. We present the design, fabrication, modeling, and testing of a personalized pneumatically-actuated soft robotic exoglove. The glove was fit to a user's hand with topological scans and fabricated with silicone mold casting. Finite element analysis (FEA) was performed to evaluate actuator bending and forces from physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) between an actuator and a simplified personalized biomechanical finger model. Pneumatic pressure control experiments were conducted to flex the user's finger with static and dynamic references. Fabrication results show that topological scans enable precise tailoring to hand anatomy. Simulations showed that anatomical personalization enables analysis of pHRI contact forces, and results indicate sufficient joint mobilization with non-ideal compression on the proximal phalanx. Pneumatic testing indicates that pressure control allows accurate and targeted mobility of the metacarpophalangeal (MCP) and proximal interphalangeal (PIP) joints with intrinsic stiffness. Testing of multiple designs showed that relaxing the strain-limiting layer improves actuator-to-finger joint alignment during actuation. This work presents personalization to the human hand in structural conformability, joint topology, modeling of pHRI contact, and time-dependent actuation-deformation profiles. This lays a groundwork for informing exoglove design optimization to enable assistance in dexterous manipulation and neuromuscular rehabilitation of fine motor skills.

</details>


### [143] [In vivo feasibility study of humanoid robots in surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07972)
*Zekai Liang,Nikita Thareja,Peihan Zhang,Calvin Joyce,Soofiyan Atar,Florian Richter,Garth Jacobsen,Shanglei Liu,Ryan Broderick,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了当前人形机器人在腹腔镜手术任务中的表现。通过构建基于人形机器人的遥操作框架，利用通用手术器械，在台面测试、不同经验水平的干实验室用户研究以及活体猪实验等多个维度进行评估，量化了技术可行性、任务表现和临床准备度，并与成熟的手术平台进行对比。研究表明人形机器人在外科手术领域具有潜力，但距离临床部署仍存在关键技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域面临人员短缺与护理需求增长之间的矛盾，而医院中大量工作本质上是具身化的（需要移动、操作和安全交互）。虽然手术机器人已有专用平台（如达芬奇手术系统），但当前人形机器人能否满足微创手术所需的精度、控制和安全要求尚不明确，因此需要进行系统性评估。

Method: 构建人形机器人腹腔镜遥操作框架，使用通用手术器械，通过三种评估方式：（1）台面性能表征；（2）涵盖不同手术经验水平的干实验室用户研究；（3）活体猪实验。与成熟手术平台进行对比评估。

Result: 量化了人形机器人在腹腔镜手术任务中的技术可行性、任务表现和临床准备度，明确了其能力边界和局限性。

Conclusion: 人形机器人在外科手术中展现出潜力，但在临床部署前仍需解决关键的技术挑战。

Abstract: Recent advances in actuation, control and learning have rapidly pushed humanoid robots from a distant vision towards near-term real-world deployment. Healthcare is a particularly pressing domain, in which staffing shortages and increasing care demand are widening the gap between clinical workload and available skilled labour. Although current automation has largely focused on digital and logistical tasks, much hospital work remains embodied, requiring mobility, manipulation and safe interaction in human-designed environments. Humanoid form factors offer unique potential, particularly for assisting with surgical tasks. Traditionally, robotic systems for surgery are purpose-built platforms such as Intuitive Surgical's da Vinci Surgical System, and it remains unclear how close current humanoid systems are to meeting the precision, control and safety requirements of minimally invasive surgery. Here we present a systematic evaluation of contemporary humanoid technology for laparoscopic surgical tasks. We develop a humanoid-based laparoscopic teleoperation framework using general-purpose instruments and assess its abilities through benchtop characterization, dry-laboratory user studies spanning diverse surgical experience levels and in vivo porcine studies. Across these evaluations, we quantify technical feasibility, task performance and clinical readiness relative to established surgical platforms. Together, our study provides an evidence-based assessment of current humanoid abilities and limitations for surgical applications, highlighting both their promise and key technical challenges that must be addressed before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [144] [D-CLIPSE: Distributed Consensus-based Localization with Passive Listening on Shared State Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07995)
*Kyle Biron-Gricken,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共识的分布式滤波框架，通过共享预积分里程计和相关共享状态，在通信高效的前提下实现接近集中式方法的多机器人定位精度和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式滤波方法虽能最优融合所有传感器测量数据，但受限于硬件、通信和计算约束难以实际部署；而现有分布式方法在一致性和精度上仍有不足。

Method: 提出了一种基于共识的分布式滤波框架，在通信机器人之间同时共享预积分里程计和相关共享状态，实现一致且通信高效的分布式状态估计。

Result: 在仿真和实验场景中，该方法在精度和一致性上均接近集中式方法性能，优于当前最先进的去中心化方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式滤波框架能够在实际部署中兼顾通信效率与估计性能，是一种可行的多机器人定位方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot localization that is accurate and consistent is imperative for downstream tasks such as planning and control. Centralized filtering approaches optimally fuse all available sensor measurements of the team. However, a centralized solution is rarely implementable due to hardware, communication, and computational constraints. Distributed approaches deploy a filter on each robot to estimate their own state and neighbours' states using inter-robot communication. This paper proposes a consistent, communication-efficient, and consensus-based distributed filtering framework that shares both preintegrated odometry and relevant shared states among communicating robots. The proposed method is validated in simulated and experimental scenarios, showing near centralized performance in accuracy, and especially in consistency, compared to the current state-of-the-art decentralized approach.

</details>
