### [145] [Factors Influencing Conversational Engagement in Robot-Delivered Individual Cognitive Stimulation Therapy (iCST) for Dementia in Home Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07998)
*Emmanuel Akinrintoyo,Nicole Salomons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究部署Co-STAR系统在八名痴呆症患者家中进行为期一周的机器人个体化认知刺激疗法（iCST）会话，分析对话指标后发现：个性化提示显著增加参与度；会话后期出现认知疲劳导致语言产出下降；首次会话指标可预测长期参与度，居住环境影响互动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人在痴呆症护理中潜力巨大，但机器人主导的认知刺激疗法（CST）中出现的对话动态特征尚不清楚，缺乏对交互模式的实证分析。

Method: 在八名痴呆症患者家中部署Co-STAR系统一周，进行30分钟iCST会话；分析每次会话的对话指标（每轮词数、语速、响应时长、响应延迟、自我参照语言），考察提示个性化、交互阶段、参与者特征对对话参与度的影响。

Result: （1）个性化提示相比通用提示显著增加响应时长、自我参照语言和整体参与度；（2）会话后期言语产出和自传体参与度下降，提示认知疲劳；（3）首次会话的对话指标与长期参与度相关，居住环境影响对话参与模式。

Conclusion: 这些发现为机器人iCST中影响对话参与度的因素提供了实证依据，有助于设计面向痴呆症疗法的自适应对话机器人。

Abstract: Social robots offer a promising means of supporting cognitive therapies for dementia care by guiding structured conversation and therapeutic activities. However, little is known about the conversational dynamics that emerge during robot-delivered cognitive stimulation therapy (CST) sessions. This study analysed the interaction patterns from robot-delivered individual CST (iCST) sessions conducted with people living with dementia in home settings. Our Co-STAR (Cognitive Stimulation Therapy by an Autonomous Robot) system was deployed in the homes of eight PwDs for one week, who completed 30-minute sessions. Conversational metrics, including words per turn, speech production rate, response duration, response latency, and self-referential language, were analysed to examine how conversational engagement is shaped by prompt personalisation, interaction phase, and participant characteristics. The findings highlight three key interactional properties of robot-delivered iCST. First, personalised prompts significantly increase response duration, self-referential language, and overall engagement compared to generic prompts. Second, conversational behaviour changes within sessions, with a reduction in the verbal output and autobiographical engagement observed during later interaction phases, which suggests cognitive fatigue. Third, first-session conversational metrics were associated with long-term participation, while living situation influenced conversational engagement patterns. These findings provide empirical insights into the factors that shape conversational engagement in robot-delivered iCST. They inform the design of adaptive conversational robots for dementia therapy.

</details>


### [146] [RadLoc: Radar-based 3-DoF Global Localization via Fast, Robust, and Lightweight Spatial Descriptor Across Diverse Environmental Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08115)
*Hogyun Kim,Jiwon Choi,Jungwoo Lee,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RadLoc，一种基于旋转雷达的端到端全局定位管线，包含从地点识别到3自由度位姿估计的完整流程。通过1D CA-CFAR加速预处理、利用近距优势设计紧凑描述子和层级化粗到精检索策略，结合相位相关位姿估计，在5个数据集15个序列上实现了最小的描述子尺寸和最快的检索速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转雷达全局定位研究多聚焦于地点识别或位姿估计等单一组件，缺乏从地点识别到位姿估计的完整且轻量的端到端方案。

Method: 1) 使用1D CA-CFAR滤波加速预处理；2) 利用旋转雷达图像近距信号占优特性设计紧凑描述子；3) 提出层级化粗到精检索策略；4) 结合相位相关方法实现3自由度位姿估计。

Result: 在5个数据集15个序列上的实验表明，RadLoc在保持鲁棒性能的同时，拥有最小的描述子尺寸和最快的检索速度，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RadLoc是一种快速、鲁棒且轻量的雷达全局定位方案，可作为通用模块适用于SLAM和多会话SLAM系统。

Abstract: While global localization using spinning radar has gained attention for its robustness to adverse weather and challenging environments, many studies have focused on individual components such as place recognition or pose estimation. In this paper, we take a holistic view of radar sensor-based global localization and present RadLoc, a fast, robust, and lightweight end-to-end pipeline from place recognition to 3-DoF pose estimation. RadLoc accelerates pre-processing using 1D CA-CFAR filtering and leverages the near-range dominance in spinning radar images to design a compact descriptor and an efficient hierarchical coarse-to-fine retrieval strategy. Moreover, coupled with phase correlation-based 3-DoF pose estimation, it forms a versatile global localization module applicable to SLAM and multi-session SLAM systems. Extensive experiments on 15 sequences across 5 datasets demonstrate that RadLoc achieves robust performance while maintaining the smallest descriptor size and fastest retrieval time among state-of-the-art approaches. The supplementary materials are available at https://sparolab.github.io/research/radloc/.

</details>


### [147] [X-ACTA: eXtended Analytic Center Tension distribution Algorithm for fixed and mobile cable-driven-parallel-robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08265)
*Domenico Dona',Vincenzo Di Paola,Alberto Trevisani,Matteo Zoppi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展解析中心方法，用于在力可行工作空间外选择缆绳张力，保证连续可微、快速收敛，且通过帕累托优势验证了其在平滑性和力误差方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缆绳张力确定方法在力可行工作空间外（如剧烈机动或缆绳故障场景）的处理能力有限，需要一种既能保持可微性、包含非线性约束，又能实现快速实时收敛的方法。

Method: 提出扩展的解析中心方法（Extended Analytic Center Method）作为在力可行工作空间外选择缆绳张力的准则，保持连续可微的张力剖面，包含非线性约束，并将力误差限制在可忽略的区域。

Result: 通过帕累托优势对比，证实了该方法在平滑性和力误差方面优于现有主流方法；数值实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效扩展缆索驱动并联机器人的工作范围，在挑战性场景中保持连续、可微、快速收敛的张力控制，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Steering Cable-Driven Parallel Robots (CDPRs) beyond their Wrench-Feasible Workspace (WFW) augments their capabilities in challenging scenarios such as during aggressive maneuvers or following a cable failure. In this context, although the determination of cable tensions is a well-studied topic, only a few approaches address these scenarios. Therefore, this paper introduces an extended version of the Analytic Center method as a criterion for selecting cable tensions outside the WFW while maintaining differentiability and including non-linear constraints. Notably, the proposed method maintains continuous and differentiable tension profiles, ensures fast real-time convergence to a unique solution, and, in contrast to other slack-based formulations, relegates wrench errors to a negligible area of the WFW. Its superiority in terms of smoothness and wrench error is confirmed via Pareto dominance with respect to the leading state-of-the-art method. Lastly, the effectiveness of the method is demonstrated through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [148] [TFP: Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies for Visuomotor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08283)
*Yushen Liang,Yue Peng,Baosheng Jin,Tianluo Zhang,Xinyu Zhang,Shuyi Zhou,Zhuoran Chen,Xinqi Liu,Shenji Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TFP（时序条件记忆融合策略），一种轻量级记忆-动作框架，通过液时恒定动力学维护任务进展信念并注入流匹配动作解码器，使VLA策略在阶段依赖操作中具备动态感知信念更新能力，在LIBERO、LIBERO-plus和MIKASA ShellGameTouch上分别提升至98.75%、93.77%和75.0%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略（如π0.5和OpenVLA）本质上是反应式的，仅依赖当前观测、指令和本体感知状态预测下一动作，在阶段依赖操作中失效——视觉相似状态可能因潜在任务进度和先前交互结果不同而需要不同动作。因此需要不仅具备记忆，还需具备动态感知信念更新能力的策略。

Method: TFP包含三个核心组件：(1) 维护片段级任务进度信念，采用液时恒定动力学（Liquid Time-Constant dynamics）实现；(2) 将更新后的信念通过自适应调制（adaptive modulation）直接注入流匹配动作解码器（flow-matching action decoder），而非仅作为被动历史上下文；(3) 使用3.3B参数模型，轻量级地集成到VLA骨干网络。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率从96.9%提升至98.75%，在LIBERO-plus上从91.4%提升至93.77%，在记忆聚焦的MIKASA ShellGameTouch诊断任务上达到75.0%成功率。机制分析显示操控事件附近的写增益变化是非事件阶段的约6倍，隐状态干预实验证明信念因果性地调制生成的动作块。

Conclusion: 紧凑的、对事件敏感的记忆动力学能够在遮挡、视觉扰动和阶段依赖任务结构下有效改进VLA策略的性能，为将动态记忆机制融入VLA框架提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) policies such as $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA perform well on many manipulation tasks, but they are often reactive: the next action is predicted from the current observation, instruction, and proprioceptive state. This assumption breaks down in stage-dependent manipulation, where visually similar states may require different actions depending on latent task progress and previous interaction outcomes. We argue that such tasks require not only memory, but dynamics-aware belief updates: the policy should preserve task progress during stable or occluded phases and revise its belief near contact, release, or subgoal transitions. We introduce Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies (TFP), a lightweight memory-action framework for VLA backbones. TFP maintains an episode-local task-progress belief with Liquid Time-Constant dynamics and injects the updated belief directly into the flow-matching action decoder through adaptive modulation. This lets temporally accumulated context shape the generated action chunk, rather than serving only as passive history context. With a 3.3B-parameter model, TFP improves the average success rate from \(96.9\%\) to \(98.75\%\) on LIBERO and from \(91.4\%\) to \(93.77\%\) on LIBERO-plus. On the memory-focused MIKASA ShellGameTouch diagnostic, TFP achieves success up to \(75.0\%\). Mechanistic analyses show that write-gain changes near manipulation events are about \(6\times\) larger than far non-event phases, and hidden-state interventions show that the belief causally modulates generated action chunks. These results suggest that compact, event-sensitive memory dynamics can improve VLA policies under occlusion, visual perturbation, and stage-dependent task structure.

</details>


### [149] [AnyDexRT: Calibration-Free Dexterous Hand Retargeting with Few-Shot Human Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08341)
*Chenxi Wang,Ying Feng,Hongjie Fang,Shangning Xia,Lixin Yang,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyDexRT是一种免标定的重定位方法，结合自监督指尖对应学习与少样本人类引导，实现跨灵巧手的直观遥操作，相比现有方法减少手动调参并提升控制效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作重定位方法依赖手工设计目标函数、精确标定或全局形状匹配，对不同灵巧手的适应性差且需要大量手动调参。

Method: 结合自监督指尖对应学习与少样本人类引导，将映射锚定在任务相关区域，并通过接触分类器进一步优化捏合姿态。

Result: 在多种灵巧手和真实遥操作任务中，AnyDexRT提升了重定位质量，减少了手动调参，提供了更直观高效的控制。

Conclusion: AnyDexRT通过免标定、自监督学习与少样本引导的联合策略，显著提升了跨灵巧手遥操作重定位的通用性与易用性。

Abstract: Teleoperation is a key interface for controlling dexterous robotic hands and collecting demonstrations for imitation learning. Its effectiveness largely depends on kinematic retargeting, which maps operator hand motions to feasible and intuitive robot hand motions. Existing methods often require hand-crafted objectives, precise calibration, or global shape matching between human and robot hand spaces, making them sensitive to hand-specific tuning and less reliable across different dexterous hands. We propose AnyDexRT, a calibration-free retargeting method for intuitive dexterous teleoperation across human-like dexterous hands. AnyDexRT combines self-supervised fingertip correspondence learning with few-shot human guidance to anchor the mapping in task-relevant regions, and further refines pinch-related poses using a contact classifier. Experiments on diverse dexterous hands and real-world teleoperation tasks show that AnyDexRT improves retargeting quality, reduces manual tuning, and provides more intuitive and efficient control than prior retargeting methods. Project website: https://chenxi-wang.github.io/projects/anydexrt

</details>


### [150] [SkillPlug: Unsupervised Skill Mining for Few-Shot Adaptation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08354)
*Zi-han Ding,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillPlug是一个插件式框架，通过技能条件模块和自监督学习构建可迁移的技能库，实现机械臂操控任务的多任务泛化和少样本快速适应，仅需微调轻量级路由器和动作头即可适配新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端视觉运动策略缺乏显式的行为复用与重组结构，导致跨任务迁移时数据效率低下，难以从少量示范中快速泛化到新任务。

Method: 提出SkillPlug框架：1) 在现有视觉运动策略上添加技能条件模块；2) 通过自监督目标从多任务示范中挖掘紧凑、可复用、非冗余的技能库作为任务共享先验；3) 固定所学技能，仅微调轻量级路由器和动作头以适应新任务。

Result: 在两组仿真基准和真实机器人上验证，挖掘的可迁移技能一致地提升了多任务性能和少样本适应能力。

Conclusion: SkillPlug提供了一种可扩展的技能挖掘方式，提升了机器人操控中数据高效的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning transferable visuomotor imitation policies that generalize across diverse manipulation tasks and adapt rapidly to new tasks from only a handful of demonstrations remains challenging. Most modern policies are trained end-to-end to map observations directly to low-level actions, offering little explicit structure for reusing and recombining behaviors across tasks and making transfer data-inefficient under limited supervision. We propose SkillPlug, a plug-in framework that augments an existing visuomotor policy with a skill-conditioning module and mines a shared, transferable skill library from raw multi-task demonstrations. SkillPlug learns skills via self-supervised objectives that promote compact, reusable, and non-redundant behavior-level primitives, forming a task-shared prior for compositional control. After skill mining, we keep the learned skills fixed and specialize to unseen tasks by fine-tuning only lightweight router and action head, enabling efficient adaptation without full end-to-end retraining. We evaluate SkillPlug on two simulation benchmarks and on a real robot, and observe that the mined transferable skills consistently improve both multi-task performance and few-shot adaptation. Overall, SkillPlug offers a scalable way to mine reusable skills that improve data-efficient generalization in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [151] [FSD-VLN: Fast-Slow Dual-System Modeling for Aerial Long-Horizon Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08359)
*Xueke Zhu,Qingyan Meng,Liutao Yu,Wei Zhang,Zhengyu Ma,Huihui Zhou,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FSD-VLN快慢双系统架构，通过解耦语义推理与低延迟飞行指令生成，在无人机视觉-语言导航任务中实现成功率翻倍、延迟降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法存在全局多模态理解与序列动作生成之间的结构错配，导致长时域空中导航出现轨迹抖动和严重决策延迟。

Method: 提出快慢双系统架构：慢流从预训练视觉-语言模型中提取稳定语义先验，快流使用扩散Transformer（DiT）建模跨时间动作分布以生成一致的飞行输出，并引入时间感知自适应优化器稳定长序列训练。

Result: 在未见场景中导航成功率比SOTA方法提升2倍，单动作推理延迟和总任务运行时间减少超过50%。

Conclusion: 验证了语义-控制解耦建模的优势，为长时域空中VLN提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables UAV autonomous navigation in unknown environments by mapping language instructions to real-time visual inputs. Compared with GPS-dependent or pre-programmed navigation, VLN supports intuitive human-machine interaction and stronger environmental adaptability, requiring tight integration of high-level semantic reasoning and low-latency flight control.Existing methods suffer from structural misalignment between global multimodal understanding and sequential action generation, causing jittery trajectories and severe decision latency for long-horizon aerial navigation. To solve this issue, we propose FSD-VLN, a fast-slow dual-system architecture disentangling semantic reasoning and low-latency flight command generation.The framework has two asynchronous branches: a slow stream extracting stable semantic priors from pre-trained vision-language models, and a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) fast stream modeling cross-temporal action distributions to produce consistent flight outputs. We further introduce a time-aware adaptive optimizer to stabilize long-sequence training and reduce gradient oscillation.Large-scale low-altitude simulation experiments show FSD-VLN achieves up to 2X higher navigation success rates on unseen scenes than SOTA methods, while cutting single-action inference delay and total task runtime by over 50%. Our work validates the benefit of decoupled semantic-control modeling and provides a practical paradigm for long-horizon aerial VLN.

</details>


### [152] [On Exploring Input Resolution Scaling For Anytime LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08391)
*Ahmet Soyyigit,Shuochao Yao,Heechul Yun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LiDAR点云3D目标检测的深度神经网络任意时间计算方法，通过单模型多分辨率推理和截止时间感知调度器，在动态操作需求下实现执行延迟与结果效用的权衡，在nuScenes数据集和自动驾驶仿真中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信息物理系统中，需要在执行延迟和结果效用之间进行权衡（即任意时间计算）以适应动态运行需求。现有方法对处理LiDAR点云的DNN模型支持不足，尤其是在保证时序约束的前提下实现高效推理。

Method: 提出多分辨率推理方法，使处理pillar或voxel点云的模型支持动态输入缩放；采用内存高效的单DNN模型部署，避免多模型资源开销；设计截止时间感知调度器，通过准确预测各分辨率执行时间，在运行时为每个输入选择最高可行分辨率。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上显著优于现有LiDAR目标检测的任意时间计算方法；在仿真自动驾驶系统中持续实现无碰撞导航，同时避免因环境复杂性导致的不必要停顿。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了LiDAR点云DNN的任意时间计算问题，为自动驾驶等实时性要求高的信息物理系统提供了可行的资源配置方案。

Abstract: Making tradeoffs between execution latency and result utility (i.e., anytime computing) for adapting to dynamic operational requirements has been shown to enhance the performance of cyber-physical systems. In this work, we focus on enabling anytime computing for deep neural networks (DNNs) that process LiDAR point clouds for 3D object detection. We propose a novel method that enables multi-resolution inference for models that process point clouds as pillars or voxels, allowing the input to be dynamically scaled and processed at the resolution needed to meet timing requirements. Importantly, our memory-efficient approach requires the deployment of only a single DNN model, avoiding the need to deploy multiple models, each trained for a different input resolution. We also introduce a deadline-aware scheduler that selects the highest possible resolution for any given input by accurately predicting the execution time for all possible resolutions at runtime, which is challenging due to the irregularity of LiDAR point clouds. Experimental results on the nuScenes autonomous driving dataset demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing anytime computing approaches for LiDAR object detection. Finally, we deploy our approach in a simulated autonomous driving system, where it consistently enables collision-free navigation while avoiding unnecessary stalls caused by environmental complexity.

</details>


### [153] [EgoWAM: World Action Models Beyond Pixels with In-the-Wild Egocentric Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08436)
*Baoyu Li,Xinchen Yin,Mengying Lin,Yixin Zhang,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoWAM框架，研究世界动作模型（WAM）中不同世界表征对从人类数据迁移到机器人操控任务的影响。实验表明，基于DINO和3D运动流的预测目标比像素级预测更能有效提升策略的泛化能力和域内性能，在双手操控任务上相比行为克隆方法有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类自我中心数据虽为机器人操控提供了可扩展的监督信号，但行为克隆会将可迁移内容（物体、场景、任务语义）与非迁移因素（人体形态、头部运动、行为风格）纠缠在一起。现有方法缺乏对如何选择世界表征以实现有效人-机迁移的系统研究。

Method: 提出EgoWAM框架，采用人类-机器人协同训练范式，固定策略主干、动作头和混合数据，仅改变世界预测目标（Pixel、DINO、3D运动流）。通过预测场景如何演化（而不仅仅是动作）来提供更好的训练信号。

Result: 在三个真实世界双手操控任务上：1) WAM协同训练比行为克隆更有效地扩展了野外人自我中心数据的利用；2) 像素级预测迁移效果弱；3) DINO提升分布外物体和场景泛化达4倍；4) 3D流提升域内性能20-30%。

Conclusion: 有效的世界目标应抽象外观、捕获与智能体无关的物理效应、分离相机运动和环境变化。DINO和3D运动流作为世界表征目标能够显著提升人-机迁移的效果。

Abstract: Egocentric human data offers scalable supervision for robot manipulation. However, behavior cloning entangles transferable content like objects, scenes, and task semantics, with non-transferable factors like human morphology, head motion, and behavioral style. We study whether World Action Models (WAMs) provide a better training signal by requiring policies to predict not only actions, but also how the scene evolves. The central question is what world representation best enables human-to-robot transfer. We hypothesize that an effective world target should abstract appearance, capture agent-invariant physical effects, and separate camera motion from environment change. We introduce EgoWAM, a controlled human-robot co-training framework that fixes the policy backbone, action head, and data mixture while varying only the world prediction target, comparing Pixel, DINO, and 3D motion flow. Across three real-world bimanual tasks, WAM co-training scales more effectively with in-the-wild egocentric human data than behavior cloning. Pixel-based prediction transfers weakly, while DINO and 3D flow yield substantial gains: DINO improves out-of-distribution object and scene generalization by up to 4x, and 3D flow improves in-domain performance by 20-30%. More details: https://gatech-rl2.github.io/egowam.github.io

</details>


### [154] [Harness VLA: Steering Frozen VLAs into Reliable Manipulation Primitives via Memory-Guided Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08448)
*Yixian Zhang,Huanming Zhang,Feng Gao,Xiao Li,Zhihao Liu,Chunyang Zhu,Jiaxing Qiu,Yuchen Yan,Jiyuan Liu,Wenhao Tang,Zhengru Fang,Yi Nie,Changxu Wei,Yu Wang,Wenbo Ding,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Harness VLA 提出一种记忆增强的智能体框架，将冻结的预训练 VLA 模型封装为可重试的接触式基元，并与少量分析基元组合，在不微调的情况下显著扩展 VLA 在分布外任务中的操作能力，在 LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa365 和 RoboTwin C2R 上分别提升 38.6、25.4 和达到 58.4 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端 VLA 模型在分布内任务轨迹上表现良好，但面对语义重定向、目标重新绑定、空间布局变化和不稳定局部接触等部署扰动时容易失败；LLM 编码智能体虽具备语义与组合推理能力，但纯分析基元难以处理不规则抓取、受限放置和铰接物体交互。现有方法无法同时兼顾精确的接触式操作与鲁棒的语义、场景及长程推理。

Method: 提出 Harness VLA 框架：将冻结的 VLA 模型作为可重试的接触式基元，与少量固定分析基元（用于接地、暂存、运输、导航和释放）组合；通过学习任务特定执行轨迹、全局成功规则与失败模型来确定这些固定基元的操作范围；将语义重新接地、非接触执行和 VLA 重新暂存提升到规划器层面，保留冻结 VLA 仅用于局部接触阶段，无需微调即可扩展预训练 VLA 的分布外能力。

Result: 在受扰动的桌面操作、家庭厨房操作及从干净到随机化的双手操作任务中，Harness VLA 在 LIBERO-Pro 上比最强基线提升 38.6 个百分点，在 RoboCasa365 上提升 25.4 个百分点，在 RoboTwin C2R 上达到 58.4%。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的智能体框架通过将预训练 VLA 作为可重试基元与少量分析基元组合，在不进行微调的前提下，有效扩展了 VLA 在分布外扰动场景下的操作能力，证明了冻结 VLA 通过外层规划器引导可超越其原始轨迹分布。

Abstract: Language-conditioned manipulation requires both precise contact-rich control and robust reasoning over language, scenes, and long horizons. End-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong local visuomotor skills, but they are trained on in-distribution task trajectories and often fail under deployment perturbations such as semantic retargeting, goal re-binding, spatial-layout shifts, and unstable local contacts. LLM coding agents provide complementary semantic and compositional reasoning, but purely analytic primitives struggle with irregular grasping, constrained placement, and articulated-object interaction. We present Harness VLA, a memory-augmented agentic framework that exposes a frozen VLA as a retryable contact-rich primitive and composes it with a small fixed library of analytic primitives for grounding, staging, transport, navigation, and release. Rather than expanding the skill library, the harness learns the operating range of these fixed primitives from task-specific execution traces, global success rules, and failure models. By lifting semantic re-grounding, non-contact execution, and VLA re-staging to the planner while reserving the frozen VLA for local contact-rich phases, Harness VLA extends pretrained VLAs beyond their original trajectory distribution without finetuning. Across perturbed tabletop, household kitchen, and clean-to-randomized bimanual manipulation, Harness VLA improves over the strongest relevant baselines by 38.6 and 25.4 percentage points on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365, respectively, and reaches 58.4% on RoboTwin C2R.

</details>


### [155] [FabriVLA: A Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Model for Precise Multi-Task Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08575)
*Shiyuan Yang,Borong Zhang,Jizheng Zhang,Zhijia Tao,Junfei Guo,Donglai Ran,Xu Bian,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FabriVLA是一个轻量级视觉-语言-动作模型，基于1B规模的InternVL3.5骨干网络和流匹配动作头，在Meta-World MT50基准上达到90.0%的平均成功率，证明紧凑型VLA无需数十亿参数即可实现强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖数十亿参数的大规模骨干网络，计算成本高昂，缺乏轻量级高效方案，作者旨在探索紧凑型VLA在精确多任务操作中的可行性。

Method: 采用InternVL3.5视觉-语言骨干网络，结合流匹配动作头（包含动作令牌间的门控自注意力和浅层VLM层融合以增强空间上下文），通过单阶段联合优化从预训练VLM和随机初始化动作头进行训练。

Result: 在Meta-World MT50（50个多样化操作任务）上，分档平均成功率达到90.0%。

Conclusion: 基于1B规模VLM构建的紧凑型VLA无需依赖数十亿参数的骨干网络即可实现强性能，为轻量级机器人操作模型提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present FabriVLA, a lightweight Vision-Language-Action model for Precise Multi-Task Manipulation. FabriVLA combines an InternVL3.5 vision-language backbone with a flow-matching action head featuring gated self-attention across action tokens and shallow VLM layer fusion for enriched spatial context. The model is trained via single stage joint optimization from a pretrained VLM and randomly initialized action head. On the Meta-World MT50 benchmark spanning 50 diverse manipulation tasks, FabriVLA achieves a tier-average success rate of 90.0%, demonstrating that a compact VLA built on a 1B scale VLM can achieve strong performance without relying on multi billion parameter VLA backbones.

</details>


### [156] [A New Human-Likeness and Comfort Index for Robot Movements Along Prescribed Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08620)
*Rosanna Coccaro,Enrico Ferrentino,Antonio Parziale,Angelo Marcelli,Pasquale Chiacchio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了人机交互中机器人运动的人类相似度与使用者舒适感之间的关系，提出基于对数正态性原理的人类相似度指数，用于在轨迹执行前评估其人类相似度，并通过68名受试者的物理交互实验验证了该指数与舒适感知的一致性趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互快速普及，但人形机器人的视觉相似度不足以保证物理交互中的接受度，接受度直接与舒适度和人体工程学相关，而舒适度取决于人类对机器人运动质量的感知。因此需要量化机器人运动与人类运动的相似程度，以预判交互舒适性。

Method: 基于人类运动建模的对数正态性原理，定义了一个人类相似度指数，用于在轨迹实际执行前对轨迹生成算法进行先验表征；通过68名受试者进行三项物理交互实验，收集受试者对舒适度的主观判断，验证该指数与舒适感知的一致性。

Result: 三项实验结果表明，建议的人类相似度指数分布与受试者的舒适度偏好之间呈现出全局一致的趋势，即指数越高，受试者感知到的舒适度越好。

Conclusion: 所提出的人类相似度指数可有效作为轨迹生成算法性能的评估指标，用于在机器人运动实际执行前判断其人类相似度，从而预测交互中的舒适度，为提升人机物理交互中的接受度提供了量化工具。

Abstract: As human-robot interaction rapidly spreads in numerous fields, the subject of robot acceptance gains increasing importance. Visual similarity to the human body, as occurs for humanoids, is generally not enough to ensure acceptance in physical interaction, as acceptance directly links to comfort and ergonomics, which are measured in terms of the quality of the robot movement perceived by the human. This paper discusses the connection between comfort and similarity of the robot movement to the human one. By considering the kinematic characterization of human movement, this paper focuses on the time laws of such movements, wherein the end-effector path is prescribed. Based on the lognormality principle for modeling human movements, a human-likeness index is defined and used to provide an a priori characterization of trajectories. Such an index can be used to evaluate the performance of trajectory generation algorithms in producing human-like movements before they are actually executed. For validation purposes, 68 subjects are required to judge their comfort. The results of three experimental campaigns involving a physical interaction with a robot demonstrate a globally consistent trend between the preference in terms of perceived comfort and the distribution of the suggested human-likeness index.

</details>


### [157] [Native Video-Action Pretraining for Generalizable Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08639)
*Qihang Zhang,Lin Li,Luyao Zhang,Shuai Yang,Yiming Luo,Shuaiting Li,Ruilin Wang,Junke Wang,Jiahao Shao,Gangwei Xu,Jiaming Zhou,Yishu Shen,Yudong Jin,Fangyi Xu,Shuailei Ma,Jiaqi Liao,Guanxing Lu,Zifan Shi,Yongkun Wen,Yujie Zhao,Weixuan Tang,Xinyang Wang,Chaojian Li,Jiapeng Zhu,Ka Leong Cheng,Nan Xue,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LingBot-VA 2.0是一个专为具身智能构建的视频-动作基础模型，通过语义视觉-动作分词器、因果预训练、稀疏MoE骨干网络和异步推理方案四项核心设计，解决了传统视频生成模型在物理环境中应用的根本缺陷，在复杂操作任务中展现出强大的少样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频生成模型是为数字内容创作设计的，在物理环境（机器人控制）中存在本质不足，亟需一种从底层为具身智能构建的视频-动作基础模型。

Method: (1) 提出语义视觉-动作分词器，替代传统重构型VAE，对齐视觉表示与语义和动作；(2) 采用因果预训练范式从头训练，避免双向架构适配时的灾难性遗忘；(3) 使用稀疏MoE骨干网络扩展模型容量而不牺牲效率；(4) 通过增强异步推理方案实现实时闭环控制，并行预测未来潜在表示与执行动作。

Result: 在真实世界的复杂操作任务部署中展现出强大的少样本泛化能力，验证了其作为鲁棒基础模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 专为具身智能设计的视频-动作基础模型LingBot-VA 2.0，通过四项核心设计创新，有效弥合了数字视频生成与物理环境控制之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The advent of video-action models offers a promising path for robot control. Nevertheless, we argue that repurposing video generative models designed for digital content creation is inherently inadequate for physical environments. To bridge this gap, we present LingBot-VA 2.0, a video-action foundation model built from the ground up for embodiment. Four core design principles showcase its evolution from LingBot-VA. (1) Departing from traditional reconstruction-focused VAEs, we introduce a semantic visual-action tokenizer, which aligns visual representations with both semantics and actions, improving instruction following and action precision in subsequent policy learning. (2) Given the strictly causal nature of temporal dynamics, we adopt a causal pretraining paradigm, training from scratch to circumvent the catastrophic forgetting that frequently occurs when adapting bidirectional architectures. (3) To meet the demands of high-frequency inference, our model employs a sparse MoE backbone, expanding model capacity without compromising efficiency. (4) Real-time closed-loop control is realized through an enhanced asynchronous inference scheme, which predicts future latents in parallel with action execution while re-grounding each rollout on the latest observation via learned forward dynamics. Real-world deployment validates LingBot-VA 2.0 as a robust foundation model, as evidenced by its few-shot generalization across complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [158] [Learning Adaptive Solvers for Distributed Factor Graph Optimization on Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08735)
*Jaeho Shin,Maani Ghaffari,Yulun Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeepCORD 是一个学习增强的分布式因子图优化框架，适用于一般矩阵李群，通过可微展开的 Riemannian 优化器学习自适应策略，在同步和异步通信模式下均优于现有分布式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式求解器依赖脆弱的 hand tuning 且主要针对刚体位姿图，缺乏对一般矩阵李群上分布式优化问题的自适应调节能力。

Method: 将并行加速的 Riemannian 优化器展开为可微迭代，学习自监督反馈策略，动态调整求解器参数以适应优化阶段和通信状态，支持同步和异步通信。

Result: 在 SE(3) 位姿图优化和 SL(4) 投影子地图对齐任务上，在大多数基准测试中比现有分布式基线取得更低的 objective 值。

Conclusion: DeepCORD 为分布式的矩阵李群因子图优化提供了一种自适应、免调参的解决方案，在多种实际场景下性能领先。

Abstract: Modern robotic perception increasingly involves large-scale geometric optimization problems distributed across multiple robots or sessions. However, existing distributed solvers often depend on brittle hand tuning and primarily target rigid body pose graphs. To address this, we present DeepCORD, a learning-augmented framework for distributed factor graph optimization on general matrix Lie groups. By unfolding a parallel and accelerated Riemannian optimizer into differentiable iterations, DeepCORD learns a self-supervised feedback policy that dynamically adapts solver parameters according to the optimization phase and communication status. The resulting method enables adaptive distributed optimization over matrix Lie groups under both synchronous and asynchronous communication regimes. Extensive experiments on real-world $\mathrm{SE}$(3) pose graph optimization and $\mathrm{SL}$(4) projective submap alignment show that our method achieves lower objective values than existing distributed baselines on most benchmarks across realistic operating scenarios.

</details>


### [159] [ContactMimic: Humanoid Object Interaction via Contact Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08742)
*Xinyao Li,Xialin He,Runpei Dong,Saurabh Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CONTACTMIMIC是一个通过跟踪显式部件级二值接触指令和关键点轨迹来解耦接触行为与关键点几何的学习框架，实现了精确物理接触和接触可控性，在10种人-物交互任务中无需任务特定奖励即可完成操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠关键点追踪不足以完成如坐椅子、擦黑板、推家具等物体交互任务，机器人可能到达正确姿态但与物体无有意义的物理接触。

Method: 提出CONTACTMIMIC框架，利用接触跟随奖励和轨迹增强方案打破关键点轨迹与接触标签之间的相关性，使策略能够解耦接触行为与关键点几何。

Result: 在10种人-物交互任务中，CONTACTMIMIC展现出接触可控性，无需任务特定奖励即可完成任务，并在接触相关任务上优于仅关键点追踪器；消融实验验证了轨迹增强方案的必要性；sim2real在5种不同运动上验证了真实世界的接触可控性。

Conclusion: CONTACTMIMIC通过显式接触指令追踪实现了精确物理接触和接触可控性（可按需产生或抑制接触），为机器人物体交互任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Keypoint tracking alone is insufficient for object interaction tasks such as sitting on a chair, wiping a board, or pushing furniture, where the robot can reach the correct pose without making meaningful physical contact with the object. We present CONTACTMIMIC, a learning framework that tracks explicit partlevel binary contact commands alongside keypoint trajectories. CONTACTMIMIC is made possible through the use of contact-following rewards and a trajectory augmentation scheme aimed at breaking the correlations between keypoint trajectories and contact labels. The resulting policy successfully decouples contact behavior from keypoint geometry, and achieves precise physical contact as well as contact-controllability (produce or suppress contact during deployment as desired). Simulation experiments across 10 diverse human-object interaction motions confirm that CONTACTMIMIC exhibits contact controllability that enables it to complete manipulation tasks without task-specific rewards, while also outperforming keypoint-only trackers on contact-relevant tasks. Ablations confirm the necessity of the proposed trajectory augmentation scheme and sim2real deployment validates contact controllability in the real world across 5 different motions. Video results are available on https://lixinyao11.github.io/contactmimic-page/.

</details>


### [160] [DexVerse: A Modular Benchmark for Multi-Task, Multi-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08751)
*Yunchao Yao,Zhuxiu Xu,Tianqi Zhang,Zixian Liu,Sikai Li,Zhenyu Wei,Feng Chen,Dihong Huang,Kechang Wan,Chenyang Ma,Shuqi Zhao,Shenghua Gao,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yi Ma,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexVerse是一个大规模模块化灵巧操作基准，包含100个任务、3种机器人手臂和6种灵巧手，支持VR遥操作和3,180条演示数据，用于评估跨任务和跨本体的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作基准在任务多样性、数据规模、本体覆盖和视觉变化可控性方面存在局限，难以系统研究跨任务和跨本体的泛化能力。

Method: 构建了包含100个任务的模块化基准DexVerse，支持3种机器人手臂和6种灵巧手，提供可配置的视觉变化（纹理、背景、光照、视角），并通过VR遥操作收集了3,180条多模态演示数据。

Result: 在19个任务上对Diffusion Policy、DP3、OpenVLA和π0.5等方法进行基准测试，揭示了现有方法在任务泛化和视觉运动鲁棒性方面存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: DexVerse可作为通用灵巧操作的有前景测试平台，弥补现有基准在跨任务和跨本体泛化研究方面的不足。

Abstract: Building general-purpose dexterous manipulation policies requires benchmarks that go beyond isolated tasks to systematically evaluate policies across diverse interaction modes, sensory conditions, and robot embodiments. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in task and data diversity, embodiment coverage, or controllable visual variation, hindering studies of cross-task and cross-embodiment generalization. We present DexVerse, a large-scale and modular benchmark for dexterous manipulation. DexVerse includes 100 tasks spanning a broad range of manipulation skills, including object grasping and relocation, articulated-object interaction, functional tool use, bimanual coordination, non-prehensile control, contact-rich behaviors, multi-goal execution, and long-horizon multi-stage task completion. It supports 3 robot arms and 6 dexterous hands, and is extensible to new tasks, assets, and embodiments. To evaluate visuomotor generalization, DexVerse provides configurable visual variations in textures, background, lighting, and camera viewpoints. We further provide a VR-based teleoperation interface and 3,180 demonstrations with synchronized proprioceptive, RGB, depth, point-cloud, and state observations. We benchmark representative methods, including Diffusion Policy, DP3, OpenVLA, and $π_{0.5}$, across 19 tasks. Results reveal substantial challenges in task generalization and visuomotor robustness, establishing DexVerse as a promising testbed for general-purpose dexterous manipulation. Project page: https://ycyao216.github.io/DexVerse.site

</details>
